# 1.21.8 protected build and native acceptance — 2026-11-09

Fabric 1.21.8 now compiles 178 adapted sources and creates a protected internal JAR. The version adapter queues the premultiplied web surface through Minecraft's GUI pipeline, converts Ultralight BGRA at upload, maps HUD transforms and tint to the queued GUI, and binds dynamic world-overlay uniforms through the native encoder. Inventory tags project the preview rotation into GUI coordinates. Glint uses vanilla texture modulation on this target, so exact recoloring parity needs visual review. No changes to those render paths on earlier targets.

Protected build and member audit passed. The member audit checked 50 targets. Real native a1 used 4,271 verified assets, the actual cosmetic agent, custom SerialGC and the isolated Rust launcher-parent fixture. Fabric a4 passed onboarding and captured eight native screenshots, including the Bat main menu, server list and live FPS, coordinates, CPS and keystrokes HUD in the disposable world. The live backgrounds render with rounded corners. Opening pause then failed because Screen.renderWithTooltip automatically invokes renderBackground, so the explicit blur in WebScreen.render queued a second blur. The 1.21.8 adapter now places the blur in the override of renderBackground and avoids duplicate vanilla darkening. Fresh Fabric a5 and Forge rebuild started. Forge private installer passed and verified its patched client. See each target's PORT-STATUS.md for evidence paths and checks still open.

# Launcher access and port verification — 2026-11-09

The user requested that production companion mods start only through Bat Client. The shared client resource bootstrap now checks the game JVM's direct parent against the PID marker supplied by the updated Rust launcher and checks its executable basename. Genuine loader development environments retain runClient. This is an offline compatibility gate. It can be imitated by a custom parent executable, so it provides no cryptographic launcher identity or tamper resistance. Existing Classic and earlier Rust builds lack the marker. The Java process API requires Java 9+, so Java 8 targets will need a separate implementation.

AdaptiveChunkBudget (Java8-compatible) observes actual world render CPU time excluding measured synchronous chunk work; fast pressure reduction/slow recovery, soft total .5–3ms allowance, 25% rebuild/75% uploads with .15/.35ms floors. ## 2026-10-24 — v118 Return Trail

User selected the proposed Return Trail through response annotation1 and explicitly requested adding it. Added optional returntrail QoL module default OFF, with dedicated route SVG, session-only native world breadcrumbs, module settings actions and optional rebindable Retrace/Resume action (unbound by default). Existing modules/enabled defaults/profiles/settings unchanged; no reset controls or launcher/site/backend changes. WEB_VERSION118. Protected AES-GCM memory-only web UI and ProGuard Java packaging retained.

Recording: TrailHistory pure Java model stores actual client-observed XYZ/time samples only. Native ReturnTrail samples every5ticks, default spacing1block (0.5–4), default lifetime10minutes (1–30), cap512points (64–1024). Jitter below spacing skipped, large >16block position jumps start a new segment; world/dimension/player changes, excluded mounted/gliding/creative-flight movement, disable/resume and death interrupt connections. Walking/jumping/swimming observations recorded; optional Only Record On Ground defaultfalse. Paused singleplayer doesn't sample. Server menu screens do not force a false route break; live observed movement can still record. Context is normalized actual save path or multiplayer address plus dimension, at most8 LRU contexts; per-context caps, expiry and cache eviction bounded. Portal switches retain separate trails within the connection; disconnect clears all session breadcrumbs. No trail files, network messages, chunk requests, terrain search, route generation or movement changes. Only module settings/keybinds persist; routes intentionally do not survive reconnect/restart. Clear On Death defaulttrue affects current context only, optional off keeps its old observations but respawn starts a new segment.

Native AFTER_ENTITIES rendering: thin normal-depth lines with backward-pointing chevrons at recorded feet+0.045. Arrows/Line/Both(default) style, color via shared picker/default#75CFFFFF, opacity80% (10–100), line width2 (1–4), range48blocks (8–128). Up to256 eligible segments rendered/frame from bounded snapshot; fade with age, skip gaps/expired/out-of-range segments, respect F1 and hide while a screen is open. Not a through-wall path or replay reconstruction. A path may be only an approximation between observed samples; not guaranteed traversability/pathfinding.

Retrace route button in Return Trail settings snapshots latest continuous section and pauses recording. Guides toward preceding breadcrumbs with a relative-direction/remaining-route-distance native top-center panel (21% vertical; optional Guide toggle). Reaching within1.5blocks advances backward; start arrival actionbar then resumes recording. Will not guide across teleport/context gaps; expiry stops outdated route. Resume recording exits guide; Clear current trail removes only active context. Fresh segment starts on resumed recording. Guide/range/color/style/lifetime/sampling settings saved normally. BatKeybinds registered trail_retrace uses Keybind Manager and Minecraft Controls, defaults unbound to avoid key conflicts. UI status shows counts and recording/retracing feedback, errors if no recorded section/disabled/no world. Native batReturnTrail bridge acts on current context only; no remote or persisted data mutations.

Minimap dots and Compass Bar dots/tracked outline, all current-dimension only. Arrival radius1–32/default5 with hysteresis; optional actionbar notification. Marker range64–30000/default10000.

User's v102 screenshot showed correct vanilla hearts but Zombie name still partly dim/hidden. Concrete issue in HealthIndicator.draw: NORMAL text pass supplied both shadow=true and nonzero background. Minecraft TextRenderer draws a shadow background at z=.01 and a second background at z=.04 after translating the foreground to z=.03; the foreground can be occluded by its own background. v102 frustum/occluded passes did not fix this. Changed to one unshadowed background/faint-label pass (SEE_THROUGH when occluded enabled, NORMAL otherwise), followed by background-free foreground/shadow, translated forward .04 label pixels. Matrix push/pop prevents offset leaking into hearts/other lines. Existing name/team eligibility, live hearts, settings, saved layout and protection unchanged. WEB_VERSION103.

Chunk diagnosis from local MC1.21.1 bytecode: WorldRenderer.updateChunks synchronously rebuilt nearby sections on render thread; ChunkBuilder.upload drained all ready GPU tasks in one call. Smooth Chunk Updates previously had no native implementation. New SmoothChunkRebuildMixin sends ordinary nearby rebuilds to Minecraft's existing async scheduler, retaining synchronous important/player-affected work while within a soft1ms budget. Excess important work also uses native async scheduler. Vanilla cancelRebuild verified to clear dirty flags only, so it does not cancel scheduled task. SmoothChunkUploadMixin limits queue polling to soft2ms per upload pass, always permitting at least one task so progress cannot starve; remaining tasks stay queued. Pure FrameWorkBudget clamps.5-8ms and finite fallbacks. Module gets saved Important Rebuild Budget .5-4/default1 and Chunk Upload Budget .5-8/default2 sliders. Existing module default on/explicit off preserved; disabled path passes vanilla scheduling/polling unchanged. No forced render/simulation distance changes, world files reset, own thread pool, synchronous disk IO changes or FPS cap. These hooks target vanilla renderer; other renderer replacements/performance-mod combinations not independently tested. Budgets reduce render-thread work spikes but do not eliminate integrated-server world generation, first-use shader/JIT work or guarantee measured FPS improvement.

Fixed UiFrameClock early-arrival jitter:2ms tolerance avoids dropping alternating frames when Minecraft frames land just before60Hz deadline. ### Combo Counter and Reach Display
IDs combo and reach; native Minecraft text/rounded translucent black backgrounds, normal shared size/shadow/style/editor positions/persistence, automatic keybind-manager toggle entries. Default positions combo x2/y38, reach x2/y42 percent. Combo settings timeout3s (1-10, .5 increments), playersOnly=false, hideInactive=false. Reach settings timeout5s (1-10), decimals2 (1-3), playersOnly=false, hideInactive=false. Preview Combo:3 / Reach:2.85 blocks; gameplay idle Combo:0 / Reach:-- unless hidden.

CombatAttackMixin injects ClientPlayerInteractionManager.attackEntity TAIL and CombatDamageMixin injects ClientPlayNetworkHandler.onEntityDamage TAIL. Read-only hooks registered in required mixins list; signatures and EntityDamageS2CPacket constructor inspected with local MC1.21.1 javap. Packet record stores actual entity IDs (constructor calls source attacker/source getId directly), no offset adjustment. No attack range, gameplay damage or packet sending altered.

CombatHud records candidate only when local non-spectator attacks a LivingEntity and at least one combat module enabled. Distance measured from local eye to EntityHitResult hit point when same target; fallback closest point on target bounding box, not target center. Candidate stores player-vs-mob filter context and monotonic time. Accepts confirmation only for same target within1500ms with sourceCauseId AND sourceDirectId matching local player; excludes projectile and unrelated/sweep-target damage. Pending candidate consumed once so duplicate packets cannot increment combo. A newer candidate supersedes older pending candidate; severely delayed/custom-server confirmations can be missed rather than guessed. This is client-measured reach paired with a vanilla server damage notification, not a server-authoritative reach audit. If a server suppresses/rewrites normal damage packets counters may not update.

Pure hud/CombatTracker owns combo/pending/last-reach timing. Confirmed same-target hits within timeout increment combo; target switch or timeout starts at1. Air swings/attempts without matching damage do not count. Any damage packet targeting local player breaks combo/pending but leaves last-reach display until its timeout. Client tick clears all across world/player instance change, disconnect and death. Config filters independently gate combo and reach; players-only optional. Reach does not change legal reach. No networking/backend/presence changes.

### Inventory HUD
ID inventory, default position x40/y60 percent, disabled. Native Minecraft items in a nine-column grid of inventory slots9-35 (27 main slots); optional hotbar appends slots0-8 as fourth row. hotbar=false, title=true, slotBackground=true, counts=true, slotGap2 (0-6). ## 2026-10-18 — v81 color picker Apply and stale-color fix

User reported wheel/picker selection did not work and requested an Apply button. Root causes in web/app.js: SV/hue/alpha drag handlers called cpPaint(false), which deliberately did not invoke cpOnChange; settings onCommit ignored the final hex passed by closeColorPicker and saved stale captured st.set[row.key]. Also hex input synchronization skipped a still-focused field, allowing stale input after programmatic reopen/interaction. Fixed all three: drags cpPaint(true), onCommit(v) saves that exact selected value via cfgSave merge, and cpPaint synchronizes the hex field on every actual picker paint. Typing itself does not repaint until change/apply, so partial editing is still possible.

Added white/black **Apply color** button in the shared picker (all module colors). applyColorPicker reads pending typed hex directly even without blur, accepts optional# and6-digit RGB/8-digit RGBA, appendsFF to6-digit, validates and shows inline status for invalid input without closing. Enter applies too; selected swatch updates, color commits to native persistence and picker closes. Existing outside-click/Escape/normal menu closing also commits final current selection for backward compatibility; this is not a new Cancel/discard workflow. Recent colors and native preview scheduling retained. cpApply now invokes live callback once instead of twice.

Resourcepack name/MOTD custom-color Apply additionally saves originalColors=false in same module patch and updates visible Keep Pack Text Colors toggle, so explicit pack styling cannot hide the selected custom color. Picker displays a hint explaining this. Background-color changes do not alter originalColors. Other module settings and unrelated modules remain merged/preserved. No native Java rendering change. style.css adds themed Apply button, focus/hover and inline note/error. WebAssetManager.WEB_VERSION81; protected/minified packaging retained.

| nameLines | 2 / 1-4 | Maximum wrapped name lines. |
| descriptionLines | 4 / 1-8 | Maximum wrapped MOTD lines. |

## 2026-10-18 — v75: Item Info, Compass, Clock, Speedometer, Keybinds, Profiles

User requested all six additions. Native HUD text/icons must use Minecraft Java rendering; menus retain existing pure black/white theme, bold UI font and fades. No blue accent theme, no PNG-icon redesign. All four new HUD modules start **disabled** and preserve explicit existing player settings. Module settings, position and scale work in both Mod Menu and the normal web HUD editor through the shared native renderer/measurement path.

### Native modules and defaults

| ID / name | Behavior and settings | Default HUD position (percent) |
|---|---|---|
| iteminfo / Item Info | Main hand (default) or Offhand; icon, localized item name, stack count, remaining/max durability and enchantments. Count/durability/enchantments default true. Hides on empty hand outside previews; editor uses diamond sword preview. Caps at 10 lines and trims wide lines to about 200px. | x40 y70 |
| compass / Compass | Heading = normalized player yaw + 180 degrees; N/NE/E/SE/S/SW/W/NW. Bar (default) scrolls cardinal markers across a 160px panel with center pointer; Simple displays heading text. Degrees default true. | x40 y3 |
| clock / Clock | Real time (default) from local system time, or Minecraft world time (world ticks + 6000, modulo 24000 converted to day seconds). 24-hour default or 12-hour; optional seconds default false. | x88 y10 |
| speedometer / Speedometer | Tick-based horizontal movement *20, optional total 3D movement; blocks/s default or km/h (*3.6), 0-2 decimals/default2. Samples reset on pause/missing player/world/dimension changes and ignore displacement >64 blocks/tick as teleport. Editor sample4.32. | x2 y25 |

Common defaults: scale1, text shadow true, background true, bgColor #0000006B and text color #FFFFFFFF. Shared rounded rectangle plate uses native Minecraft text and item renderer. Item icon, text, scaled measurements and editor hitboxes come from the same code. ExtraHud.tick is called from BatClientMod's client tick. Native fallback HudEditorScreen remains an older ghost-box prototype; the normal editor is the web editor/native HUD combination.

### Keybind manager

Added KEYBINDS and PROFILES tabs beside Mod Menu through client-tools.js, loaded after app.js in index.html, overlay.html, pause.html and respacks.html. Uses existing font/theme, rounded controls, transitions and responsive scrolling. Keybind manager offers search, keyboard/mouse capture, clear, reset, binding captions and Bat/Minecraft control collision warnings.

BatKeybinds registers native menu/zoom/perspective controls. Menu is custom-bindable; Zoom is hold; Perspective cycles on press; other module bindings toggle enabled state. Physical GLFW key/mouse polling and rising-edge tracking keeps zoom functional even with a conflicting vanilla key such as C/save-hotbar. No module toggle while a screen is open or player is absent. Focus respected. Capturing suppresses held-key edges, Escape cancels capture, and closing/leaving the manager cancels capture. Existing one-Escape close behavior otherwise unchanged. Profile actions prime held controls to avoid accidental reopening/toggling.

Zoom/Perspective use module set.key; 'Minecraft Controls' means the current native bound key. Other Bat actions use root bindings. Clearing uses key.keyboard.unknown, reset removes custom binding. Vanilla Minecraft controls remain global and are not overwritten by profiles. Describe API returns effective Bat and Minecraft controls for warnings. Binding-only web edits materialize default module enabled state through cfgSave, **not full saveModState**, preserving native anchor/offset positions. This had a dedicated regression test.

### Profiles and persistence

Profiles are local, stored in the existing atomic config/batclient-hud.json. Schema:

```json
{"modules":{},"bindings":{},"activeProfile":"Default","profiles":{"Default":{"modules":{},"bindings":{}}}}
```

Each profile snapshots only modules and bindings, never recursively snapshots profiles. This preserves enabled states, x/y/anchor/offsets, scale, all per-module settings and Bat bindings. The active profile auto-synchronizes on persistent writes. Existing configs migrate into Default when profiles are first initialized, without resetting prior settings. Create clones current settings and activates; Load saves prior active state then restores target; Rename renames active; Delete accepts inactive profiles only. Limit16 profiles, names1-48 characters, control characters rejected. Names are JSON object keys, never file paths; DOM textContent prevents name markup injection. Failures return visible errors, not false success. Native API unavailable in browser preview shows a message rather than pretending it saved.

Frequent editor feed excludes the profiles field **before** deep-copying other config fields, avoiding repeated copying of every saved profile. Profile data, accounts, skin/cape credentials and backend state do not go into the HUD feed. Existing atomic writes/preview flushing and unrelated-module merge behavior retained.

## 2026-10-18 — v76 Motion Blur

Added motionblur / Motion Blur in QoL, default **off**, adjustable strength0-85/default40. Native GPU effect rather than a web image overlay. Uses the existing module persistence/profile/keybind system; no draggable HUD widget for this world effect.

- MotionBlurMixin injects GameRenderer.render HEAD for inactive cleanup, renderWorld RETURN for accumulation after world/hand and before HUD/crosshair/UI, and close HEAD for GPU disposal. Registered in required batclient.mixins.json; MC1.21.1 method signatures checked with local javap, compiled successfully.
- MotionBlur.java creates a GLSL150 fullscreen triangle using gl_VertexID, a sampler and weight uniform, one RGBA8 history texture and VAO/program. Blend history over current world, then glCopyTexSubImage2D stores accumulated world frame. No CPU pixel readback or per-frame image upload in production. One texture consumes approximately width*height*4 bytes plus driver overhead; enabled effect costs GPU bandwidth/draw/copy work and is not an FPS improvement.
- Temporal weight = pow(clamp(strength/100,0,.85), elapsedSeconds*60), so decay is consistent over real time at different FPS. Nonfinite strengths/times, nonpositive elapsed or gaps>.25s bypass blending. Strength0 draws nothing. First valid frame seeds history. World identity changes, resize and long gaps invalidate old history. Screen open/pause/unfocus/disabled releases texture and resets world/time/history; next gameplay frames reseed rather than smear menu pixels.
- Saves/restores raw GL program, VAO, active texture unit/texture0 binding, unpack buffer, read/draw framebuffer, separate RGB/alpha blend funcs/equations, depth/cull/scissor/blend enable flags and viewport. Unpack buffer set0 during allocation prevents an unrelated PBO from interpreting the null allocation pointer as a buffer offset. RenderSystem cache remains consistent because raw state is restored. Does not modify projection, cursor/crosshair or HUD rendering.
- On shader compile/link/runtime initialization exception, logs [motionblur] and disables effect for session. Deletes intermediate shader handles on failures/success. Renderer close deletes history texture/program/VAO. No unsupported claim of compatibility with every shader pack/render mod; in-game comparison and shader-mod matrix remain pending.
- web/app.js adds module/card/description/strength slider, SVG icon and preview label using current theme/font. No fake blurred HUD preview. WebAssetManager.WEB_VERSION76 ensures current packaged assets next launch. Java remains obfuscated and web remains minified; no readable batclient-web extraction introduced.

## Project overview

`bat-client-mod/` is the **companion Fabric mod** for Bat Client (the Electron launcher at the repo root). Its flagship feature: the Bat-branded **main-menu takeover** — a Feather-style web UI rendered by an embedded **Ultralight** engine that replaces the vanilla Minecraft title screen. The web menu's buttons bridge into Java to open the real MC screens (singleplayer/multiplayer/settings/quit). WIP companion client (`batclient.client.json`): HUD modules, custom screens, etc.

- MC version: **1.21.1** (fabric), yarn `1.21.1+build.3`, loader `0.16.9`, fabric-api `0.102.1+1.21.1`.
- Gradle: loom `1.17.21`, Gradle wrapper `9.6.1`, JDK 21 toolchain.
- Engine: **Ultralight SDK** via `io.github.nilsen84:ultralight-bindings:1.0.0-SNAPSHOT` (repsy) + `:windows-x64` classifier. Dead ends (do NOT retry): UJR snapshots purged, `com.labymedia` needs proprietary natives, Lunar WebCore unusable.
- The bindings jar in gradle cache: `~\.gradle\caches\modules-2\files-2.1\io.github.nilsen84\ultralight-bindings\1.0.0-SNAPSHOT\234480696c25705aac632367973c1daa47098116\ultralight-bindings-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar`. No sources jar exists — inspect APIs via `javap -p`/`javap -v` (Kotlin metadata `d2` strings reveal param names).
- Packaging: loom `include` base + `windows-x64` binding jars → `META-INF/jars/`.

## Repo layout (this mod)

| Path | Role |
|---|---|
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/BatClientMod.java` | Entry (`onInitializeClient`), preload thread (`loadNatives`), tick takeover (TitleScreen → WebScreen), Right Shift keybind, `ClientLifecycleEvents.CLIENT_STOPPING` → `WebController.closeWindow()` + `cleanStaleNatives()`. |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/WebController.java` | Singleton: `loadNatives()` (background; loads DLLs), `cleanStaleNatives()` + `deleteRecursively()` (temp-dir leak fix, added 2026-09-30), `ensureRenderer()` (render thread only; creates `UltralightRenderer` with `BatWebFileSystem`/`BatWebLogger`/`BatWebClipboard`), `updateAndRender()` (per frame: `renderer.update()+refreshDisplay()+render()` then `window.renderAndUpload()`), `createScreen()`, `closeWindow()`, `currentWindow()`, static `framebufferWidth()/Height()`. |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/WebWindow.java` | Wraps one `UltralightView`: GL texture + **PBO upload**, dirty-region staging copy, input forwarding, `draw()` full-screen quad via `batclient:bgra_position_tex`. |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/WebScreen.java` | MC `Screen` hosting the view: `initView()`, `bindBridge()` (JS↔Java), `handleAction`, input overrides, `shouldCloseOnEsc()`, `shouldPause()`, `close()/removed()` → `closeWindow()`. |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/WebAssetManager.java` | Extracts `assets/batclient/web/**` from classpath into `<gameDir>/batclient-web` for a real `file://` URL. `WEB_VERSION` marker (`"12"`) + `WEB_FILES` allowlist. |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/BatWebFileSystem.java` | Ultralight `FileSystem` bridge → serves files from `batclient-web` (path-confined to web root). |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/BatWebLogger.java` | Ultralight `log()` → SLF4J logger `BatClient-Ultralight`. Does NOT capture page `console.*`; JS console comes via `UltralightViewListener.onAddConsoleMessage`. |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/BatWebClipboard.java` | System clipboard bridge (GLFW window clipboard). |
| `src/main/java/com/batclient/web/BatClientShaders.java` | Registers core shader `batclient:bgra_position_tex` (BGRA→RGB swap, premultiplied alpha). |
| `src/main/resources/assets/batclient/web/{index.html, style.css, app.js, bg.png, bat-logo.png}` | The menu UI (Feather-style). No `batclient.client.json` exists in the current tree (WIP never landed). |
| `src/main/resources/assets/batclient/shaders/core/bgra_position_tex.{fsh,json}` | The BGRA8→RGB fragment shader + JSON descriptor (blend add/srcalpha, vertex `position_tex`, uniform `ColorModulator`). |
| `src/main/resources/assets/batclient/lang/en_us.json` | `key.batclient.openSettings` = "Bat Client Settings", `category.batclient.title` = "Bat Client". |
| `build.gradle` / `gradle.properties` / `settings.gradle` | loom 1.17.21, MC 1.21.1, yarn 1.21.1+build.3, loader 0.16.9, fabric-api 0.102.1+1.21.1; Gradle wrapper 9.6.1; Java 21 toolchain; ultralight-bindings `implementation`+`include`+`runtimeOnly(windows-x64)`+`include`. |
| `run/` | Dev game dir: `batclient-web/` (extracted assets + version.txt), `logs/latest.log`, `runclient-test*.txt` (redirected console), `compile-out.txt` (compile redirect), saves (e.g. "New World"), plus standard MC dirs (mods/, config/, resourcepacks/, data/, crash-reports/, options.txt, servers.dat, usercache.json). |

## Build & run workflow (VERIFIED)

- Build jarmod: `.\gradlew.bat build` → BUILD SUCCESSFUL (empty `:test` is fine for the mod's own Gradle; the AGENTS "dist fails" note is about the launcher, not this mod).
- Quick compile check: `.\gradlew.bat compileJava --console=plain -q` → EXIT=0.
- Dev run: `.\gradlew.bat runClient --console=plain -q > run\runclient-testNN.txt 2>&1` launched via `Start-Process cmd.exe /c ... -WindowStyle Hidden` so it survives the shell tool timeout (~MC load ≈ 60-90s; poll `run\logs\latest.log` for `[bat] init ok` — deterministic readiness marker printed by the page's own JS).
- BETWEEN RUNS: `taskkill /F /IM java.exe /IM javaw.exe` (Stop-Process sometimes triggers a shell "ChildProcess.kill" error — taskkill is reliable). Delete `run\logs\latest.log` and any `run\hs_err_pid*.log` before relaunching.
- Loom generates a RANDOM dev username each run (e.g. Player536, Player268) — the web pill shows the real session name when the bridge works.

## Web asset extraction (CRITICAL GOTCHA)

`WebAssetManager`:
- `WEB_FILES` array is an **ALLOWLIST** — a packaged file NOT in it is never copied (bat-logo.png was missing → "logo isn't working"; fixed 2026-09-30 by adding it).
- `extract()` **skips any target file that already exists and is non-empty**. So editing a web asset WITHOUT bumping `WEB_VERSION` = the change is silently ignored (the avatar stayed blurry for exactly this reason — stale app.js was served). Any web asset change (html/css/js/png) MUST bump `WEB_VERSION`.
- `version.txt` holds the marker; mismatch → the dir is wiped and rebuilt.
- Current `WEB_VERSION = "12"`.

## Native DLL extraction & the temp-dir leak (FIXED 2026-09-30)

ultralight-bindings' `UltralightLoader.load()` ALWAYS does this on `loadNatives()` (verified via `javap -c` on the bindings jar):

1. For each native lib name it builds a resource path `/io/github/nilsen84/ultralight/natives/<folder>/<prefix><name>.<ext>` and resolves it with `Class.getResource(...)`. If missing → `IllegalStateException("Missing native library: ...")`.
2. `Files.createTempDirectory("ultralight-internal-natives-")` — a **brand new unique `%TEMP%` dir on EVERY JVM run**.
3. Copies 5 DLLs into it (`Sph0.dll`, `Sph1.dll`, `Sph2.dll`, `Sph3.dll`, `ultralight-jni.dll` — only Windows-x64 natives exist; total ~45 MB), then `System.load(absPath)` each.

**There is NO deleteOnExit / shutdown hook / cleanup in the library.** In dev we found 31 duplicate `ultralight-internal-natives-*` dirs (~44.8 MB each ≈ 1.36 GB total) accumulated from 31 `runClient` launches. Normal players hit the same leak: ~45 MB of fresh temp dir per game launch, never reclaimed.

Note: it is a *copy*, not a network download — the DLLs ship INSIDE the mod jar (loom `include` → `META-INF/jars/`, exposed to `Class.getResource` by Fabric's nested-jar classloader). So every player always has them, extraction is local each launch, but it still runs every time.

**Fix (WebController):** sweep leftover dirs at startup BEFORE loading, plus best-effort on shutdown. Because a prior run's DLLs are only locked while its JVM is alive, the sweep deletes everything except the current process's own dir (which is DLL-locked and stays for the next-run sweep). Steady state = at most ONE ~45 MB dir, never a pile.

```java
public static void cleanStaleNatives() {
    String tmp = System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir");
    if (tmp == null || tmp.isEmpty()) return;
    File base = new File(tmp);
    File[] dirs = base.listFiles((d, name) -> name.startsWith("ultralight-internal-natives-"));
    // ... deleteRecursively(d) each; LOGGER.info "Cleaned stale Ultralight natives dir: {}"
}
```

- `loadNatives()` calls `cleanStaleNatives()` right before `UltralightLoader.INSTANCE.load()`.
- `BatClientMod` CLIENT_STOPPING: `WebController.INSTANCE.closeWindow(); WebController.cleanStaleNatives();`
- Verified live (test29): planted 3 fake `ultralight-internal-natives-fake1..3` dirs → launch → log shows `Cleaned stale Ultralight natives dir: ...fake1/2/3` then `Ultralight natives loaded`; fakes gone, exactly one real dir remains.
- AVOID `\u` in comments: javac rejects `%TEMP%\ultralight` in a Javadoc as "illegal unicode escape" (`\u` is the escape prefix). Write plain "temp dir named ultralight-internal-natives-*".

## The rendering pipeline (all Kepler/NVIDIA gotchas RESOLVED)

Rendering in 1.21.1: `Tessellator.getInstance().begin(DrawMode.QUADS, VertexFormats.POSITION_TEXTURE)` → `.end()` → `BufferRenderer.drawWithGlobalProgram(...)`; shader `batclient:bgra_position_tex` (BGRA premultiplied).

Sound-killing DL: the old Kepler driver needs ALL pixel-store state reset — MC's own texture uploads leave `GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH`/`SKIP_*` dirty → "Invalid PBO operation" (1282) on PBO upload + over-read AVs on client-memory uploads. The fix in `WebWindow.renderAndUpload()`:
- Before upload: `glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,1)`, `GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH=0`, `GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS=0`, `GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS=0`.
- Upload via PBO sized EXACTLY to `dirtyBytes` per frame (`glBufferData` orphan + `glBufferSubData` + `glTexSubImage2D(..., 0L)`); surface pixels are staged copy into a padded `ByteBuffer` (dirty-region rows) before hitting GL, so the async software compositor never hands the driver freed memory.
- Success signature: diag lines show `bufData=0x0 subData=0x0 texSub=0x0 unpackRowLength=0`, `pboSize==need`, 0 GL errors, incremental small dirty rects (first frame = full 854x480, then hover/repaint rects).

## The JS bridge (ROOT CAUSE of "buttons don't work" — FIXED)

Two independent bugs were stacked:

1. **`bindFunction` called before the JS context existed.** `bindBridge()` ran in `initView()` right before `loadUrl()`, so `window.batAction`/`window.batGetPlayer` were never attached → **`typeof window.batAction === 'undefined'` forever** → every click did nothing. Confirmed by the Java-side probe: `bridge probe: undefined|-|undefined`.
   FIX: attach the bridge in a load listener:
   ```java
   window.view().setLoadListener(new UltralightLoadListener() {
       @Override public void onWindowObjectReady(String url) { bindBridge(); }
   });
   ```
   `onWindowObjectReady` fires when the first JS context is created, BEFORE page scripts run — binds le, then all page code sees the natives.
   After fix, probe reads: `bridge probe: function|[object JavaCallback]|function`.

2. **Global function name shadowing (defense-in-depth).** `app.js` declared top-level `function batAction()` / `function batGetPlayer()` — the SAME names as the native bindings. A global script function declaration can replace `window.batAction`, turning the wrapper into infinite self-recursion (RangeError) or killing script instantiation. Fixed by renaming page helpers to `callBat()` / `getSessionName()` (never call them `batAction`/`batGetPlayer`!). They call the natives via `if (typeof window.batAction === "function") try { window.batAction(action); }`.

3. **`handleAction` used to call `WebController.closeWindow()` from INSIDE the view's own JS callback** (`view.close()` mid-callback can abort the chain). REMOVED — teardown is handled by `WebScreen.removed()` (on setScreen) and `ClientLifecycleEvents.CLIENT_STOPPING` (on quit). Never destroy the view from within its own `batAction` callback.

### JS console capture
`UltralightViewListener.onAddConsoleMessage(MessageLevel, String message, int lineNumber, int columnNumber, String sourceId)` routes page `console.log` into the SLF4J log tagged `[web:js]` (e.g. `[web:js] Log:27 [bat] click singleplayer`). This is the ONLY way to see JS console output (BatWebLogger does not capture it).

### Input API signatures (verified via Kotlin metadata `d2` param names)
- `fireMouseMoveEvent(int button, int x, int y)` — WebWindow passes `pressedButton()`.
- `fireMouseButtonEvent(int button, boolean down, int x, int y)` — button index 0/1/2 (GLFW LEFT/RIGHT/MIDDLE), MC GUI-unit coords multiplied by `xScale` (framebuffer/scaledWidth).
- `fireScrollEvent(int deltaY)` — wheel * 50.
- `fireKeyEvent(KeyEvent)` with `LwjglKeyMapper.toUltralight(glfwKey)`; synth char for Enter/Tab.

### handleAction destinations (current)
- `singleplayer`: if `client.getLevelStorage().getLevelList().iterator().hasNext()` (any worlds) → `SelectWorldScreen(new TitleScreen())`; else `CreateWorldScreen.create(client, new TitleScreen())`. (User wanted: world list first, creation only when no worlds.) Uses `LevelStorage.LevelSave`, NOT `LevelSummary` (verified via javap on the mapped jar).
- `multiplayer`: `MultiplayerScreen(new TitleScreen())`.
- `settings`: `OptionsScreen(new TitleScreen(), client.options)`.
- `quit`: `client.scheduleStop()`.
- Each wrapped in `client.execute(...)` (from the JS callback thread, schedule onto the client thread).

### Screen/window lifecycle (WebScreen)
- `initView()`: closes any old view, sizes the new `WebWindow(renderer, fbW, fbH)` to the framebuffer, sets `windowContentScaleCallback(scale, scale)` where `scale = fbW / scaledWidth` (physical-pixel ratio for input coords), installs the load listener (`onWindowObjectReady` → `bindBridge()`) and view listener (`onAddConsoleMessage` → `[web:js] {level}:{line} {message}`), then `loadUrl(webRoot/index.html file URI)`.
- Mouse: `mouseMoved`/`mouseDragged` → `onCursorPos`; `mouseClicked`/`mouseReleased` → `onMouseButton(button, PRESS/RELEASE, x, y)` (logs `[bat] click recv x= h y= btn=`); `mouseScrolled` → `onScroll` (deltaY = vertical*50).
- Keyboard: `keyPressed` (ESC + `shouldCloseOnEsc()` → `this.close()`), `keyReleased`, `charTyped` → `onCharMods`.
- `shouldPause()` false so the game keeps ticking. `shouldCloseOnEsc()` = `world != null` — on the main menu ESC must NOT close (MC turns `setScreen(null)` with no world into a fresh TitleScreen → takeover tick rebuilds the window ~20/s). In-game overlay ESC closes.
- `close()` / `removed()` both call `WebController.INSTANCE.closeWindow()`, null the window, and `BatClientMod.setActiveScreen(null)`.

### Input forwarding (WebWindow → Ultralight)
- `onCursorPos(x,y)`: stores `lastMouseX/Y = x*xScale`, captures `pressedButton()` (GLFW L/R/M held), calls `view.fireMouseMoveEvent(button, (int)lastMouseX, (int)lastMouseY)`.
- `onMouseButton(glfwButton, action, x, y)`: maps GLFW LEFT/RIGHT/MIDDLE → Ultralight index 0/1/2 (else 3), `down = action==PRESS`, `fireMouseButtonEvent(button, down, x*xScale, y*xScale)`.
- `onScroll`: `fireScrollEvent((int)(vertical*50))`.
- `onCharMods(codepoint, mods)`: `KeyEvent(Type.Char, modifiers(mods), 0,0, text, text, ...)`.
- `onKey(glfwKey, scanCode, action, mods)`: PRESS→KeyDown / RELEASE→KeyUp; `LwjglKeyMapper.INSTANCE.toUltralight(glfwKey).getCode()` for virtualKey; synthesizes a Char KeyEvent for Enter (`\n`) and Tab (`\t`) since MC sends no char callback for them.
- `modifiers(int)` maps GLFW shift/ctrl/alt/super → `EnumSet<KeyEvent.Modifier>`.
- `pressedButton()` queries GLFW for the held mouse button (drag events), 3 = none.

### Web menu UI DOM (index.html)
- `<header class="brand" id="main-brand">` = `img.brand-logo` (bat-logo.png) + `span.brand-text` "BAT CLIENT".
- `<nav class="main-menu">` 5 `button.menu-item[data-action]`: singleplayer / multiplayer / cosmetics / settings / quit-game.
- `.user-pill` → `canvas#user-avatar` + `span#user-name` + 2 `button.pill-btn[data-action]` (cosmetics 🦇 svg, settings gear svg).
- `.version` = "Bat Client 0.1.0".
- `.panel-layer.hidden` → `aside.sidebar` (brand dot + name, `.nav-item[data-view]` settings/cosmetics/mods, `.back-btn[data-action=back]`) + `main.content` → `#headline`, `section.card#view-settings` (FPS Counter/Coordinates/RGB toggles), `section.card#view-cosmetics` (Animated Cape toggle), `section.card#view-mods` (Sodium/Lithium toggles), `.status#status`.
- `script src="app.js"` at end.

### Web menu JS (app.js) — see "The JS bridge" for the name-shadowing rule
- `callBat(action)` / `getSessionName()` wrappers (NEVER name them `batAction`/`batGetPlayer`).
- `[data-action]` click handler: singleplayer/multiplayer/settings/quit → `callBat(...)`, cosmetics → `openPanel("cosmetics")`, back → `closePanel()`.
- `.pill-btn` click: cosmetics → `openPanel`, settings → `callBat("settings")`.
- Panels: `views = ["settings","cosmetics","mods"]`, `headlines` map, `openPanel`/`closePanel`/`switchView` toggle `.hidden` + `.nav-item.active`; `closePanel` re-adds `active` to `#main-brand`.
- Toggles: `.switch input` change → `#status` shows "✓/✗ <id> enabled/disabled" (cosmetic placeholders only; no persistence).
- `drawAvatar(name)`: `hashString` (Java-style `h = (h<<5)-h + c |0`, `Math.abs`) picks skin/hair tone + eye color; renders an 8×8 MC head into a **40×40 canvas (5× integer scale) with `imageSmoothingEnabled=false`**, CSS 40×40 — this is the crisp-avatar fix (Ultralight ignores `image-rendering:pixelated`, so 8×8 stretched to 34px was a blur).
- init: `playerName = getSessionName() || "BatPlayer"`, set `#user-name`, `drawAvatar`, `console.log("[bat] init ok, player="+playerName)` (the readability marker for dev runs).

### Menu styling (style.css) final values (WEB_VERSION "12")
- `.menu-page` body: block, `background:#05070d`, `height/width 100vh/vw`, `overflow:hidden`.
- `.bg-sky`: fixed inset 0, `bg.png` cover + `::after` vignette (radial 32% 42% + vertical gradient).
- `body.menu-page .brand`: `position:fixed; left:18px; top:calc(50% - 252px)` (~59px above SINGLEPLAYER top = `50% - 105px`), `gap:18px; align-items:center` — the user-approved position.
- `.brand-logo`: 84×84, `object-fit:contain`, drop-shadow.`.brand-text`: 30px, weight 800, `letter-spacing:5px`, white + text-shadow.
- `.main-menu`: `left:58px; top:50%; translateY(-38%)`, column, `gap:4px`. `.menu-item`: 25px, weight 700, `letter-spacing:3.5px`, `padding:11px 0`, faint white (`rgba(255,255,255,.55)`); hover/`active`: white, `translateX(10px)`, glow. NO button has the `active` class (the leftover one on SINGLEPLAYER was removed — it made the button look non-interactive).
- `.user-pill`: fixed right 26 / top 22, blur backdrop, rounded 14px. `.user-avatar`: 34→40px is set inline by JS (`canvas.style.width/height = "40px"`); CSS keeps `border-radius:8px; image-rendering:pixelated` (harmless). `.user-name` 14px.
- `.version`: fixed left 26 / bottom 18, 13px, faint.
- `.panel-layer`: fixed inset 0, `display:flex`, `background:rgba(14,14,21,.97)`, z-index 5. `.hidden { display:none !important }` (the `!important` defeats the same-specificity `display:flex` from `.panel-layer` — a `.hidden{display:none}` plain rule lost and left the panel open).

### The 5 menu files (sizes in the jar at time of writing)
`app.js` 5293 B · `bat-logo.png` 48811 B (500×500, copied from repo root) · `bg.png` 40725 B · `index.html` 6744 B · `style.css` 6656 B. Extract check: hash the src asset vs `run/batclient-web` — they must match (a mismatch means a web edit happened without a WEB_VERSION bump).

## Web menu UI state (2026-09-30)

- Layout `.menu-page`: `bg.png` cover + vignette overlay; brand (logo + "BAT CLIENT") top-left; menu column at `left:58px; top:50%; translateY(-38%)` (5 buttons, gap 4px, padding 11px 0, font 25px → ~276px tall; SINGLEPLAYER top = `50% - 105px`).
- `.brand`: `position:fixed; left:18px; top:calc(50% - 252px)` (≈59px above SINGLEPLAYER), `gap:18px; align-items:center`.
- `.brand-logo`: `width/height: 84px; object-fit:contain; drop-shadow`. Uses **`bat-logo.png`** (500x500 copied from repo root) — `<img class="brand-logo" src="bat-logo.png">`. (Was an inline SVG silhouette; user asked for the PNG logo.)
- `.brand-text`: `font-size:30px; letter-spacing:5px`.
- `.user-pill` top-right: avatar canvas + username + 2 pill buttons (cosmetics 🦇 svg, settings gear) that call `callBat("settings")` / `openPanel("cosmetics")`.
- Buttons: `[data-action]` singleplayer/multiplayer/cosmetics/settings/quit. ALL five share `class="menu-item"` (no `active` — a leftover `active` on SINGLEPLAYER made it white+translateX+glow and is what the user meant by "singleplayer isn't like other button").
- **Avatar crispness**: `drawAvatar()` renders the hash-generated 8x8 MC head at **5x integer scale = 40x40** canvas with `imageSmoothingEnabled=false`, CSS 40px. (Before: 8x8 stretched to 34px = browser-smoothed blur — Ultralight ignores `image-rendering:pixelated`.)
- `.panel-layer` had a CSS bug: `display:flex` after `.hidden{display:none}` (same specificity) → settings panel always covered the menu. Fixed with `.hidden{display:none !important}`.
- ESC: `WebScreen.shouldCloseOnEsc()` returns `MinecraftClient.getInstance().world != null` — on the main menu ESC must NOT close (MC turns `setScreen(null)` with no world into a fresh TitleScreen → takeover tick rebuilds the window ~20/s). In-game overlay ESC closes.
- `shouldPause()` returns false.
- Additional detail cross-reference: the full DOM / JS / CSS listing lives above under "Web menu UI DOM (index.html)", "Web menu JS (app.js)", "Menu styling (style.css) final values (WEB_VERSION "12")", and "The 5 menu files".

## Shutdown crash (0xC0000409) note

A `0xC0000409` exit on quit was seen pre-hook. `ClientLifecycleEvents.CLIENT_STOPPING.register(client -> WebController.INSTANCE.closeWindow())` was added so `view.close()` runs before WebKit teardown. The 13:50 run quit cleanly ("Stopping!") without a new hs_err — but this was not exhaustively A/B verified; keep an eye on it after future runs. `CloseWindow` inside the JS callback was removed (see bridge notes) which also reduces this risk.

## Diagnostic log lines (what to grep in run/logs/latest.log)

| Pattern | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `[bat] init ok, player=X` | Page JS ran; bridge present (X = real session name) |
| `bridge probe: function|[object JavaCallback]|function` | window.batAction/batGetPlayer are native bindings (success); `undefined|-|undefined` = bindBridge didn't attach yet |
| `[web:js] Log:NN [bat] click <action>` | Page received a button click |
| `[bat] web action: <action>` | Java `handleAction` ran (setScreen dispatched) |
| `[bat] click recv x=.. y=.. btn=..` | `WebScreen.mouseClicked` received the click |
| `upload first-frame: view=854x480 ...` + `upload diag #0..#4` with all `=0x0` | GL upload healthy (first 5 frames only) |
| `[ultralight] Creating View ... width: 854 height: 480` | View created; page URL logged right after |
| `Cleaned stale Ultralight natives dir: <path>` | native temp-dir sweep removed a previous run's dir (natives leak fix) |
| `Ultralight natives loaded` | `UltralightLoader.load()` succeeded (after the sweep) |

## Key technical references

- Referenced working UJR mod studied at `$env:TEMP\opencode\refmods\fabric-ultralight-mod` (different library: `net.janrupf.ujr`, but upload technique informed our PBO/staging path).
- Bindings jar (for javap): `~\.gradle\caches\modules-2\files-2.1\io.github.nilsen84\ultralight-bindings\1.0.0-SNAPSHOT\234480696c25705aac632367973c1daa47098116\ultralight-bindings-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar`. Natives classifier jar: same path, hash dir `59b167f3d9163f1723fa1d6cecf5ffe66c19a665`, `...-windows-x64.jar` (contains `/io/github/nilsen84/ultralight/natives/windows-x64/{Sph0,Sph1,Sph2,Sph3,ultralight-jni}.dll`).
- `UltralightLoader.INSTANCE.load()` bytecode (javap -c): resolves each native via `Class.getResource("/io/github/nilsen84/ultralight/natives/<folder>/<prefix><lib>.<ext>")` → throws `IllegalStateException("Missing native library: ...")` if null → `Files.createTempDirectory("ultralight-internal-natives-")` → `URL.openStream()` + `Files.copy` into it → `System.load(absPath)`. NO delete/deleteOnExit/shutdown-hook anywhere → per-run leak (fixed by our sweep). `Bindings` enum = WINDOWS_X64 (folder, extension, prefix); internal `UltralightLoaderKt.NATIVES` list.
- Mapped jar for API verification: `~\.gradle\caches\fabric-loom\minecraftMaven\net\minecraft\minecraft-merged\1.21.1-net.fabricmc.yarn.1_21_1.1.21.1+build.3-v2\minecraft-merged-1.21.1-net.fabricmc.yarn.1_21_1.1.21.1+build.3-v2.jar` — `javap -p` on it when unsure of any MC API (e.g. `LevelStorage.getLevelList()` iterates `LevelSave`, NOT `LevelSummary`).
- `UltralightRenderer` has no destroy/close; `UltralightView` is `AutoCloseable`. Renderer teardown at shutdown isn't possible via API.
- Ultralight JS probe idiom:
  ```java
  String probe = view.evaluateScript("(function(){ try { var t = typeof window.batAction; var s = t === 'function' ? String(window.batAction) : '-'; return t + '|' + s.slice(0,48) + '|' + (typeof window.batGetPlayer); } catch(e){ return 'ERR:'+e; } })()");
  ```
  (a native binding stringifies as the JNI callback object, never the page function).

## Build & package details

- `build.gradle`: plugins `fabric-loom 1.17.21` + `maven-publish`; repos mavenCentral + repsy (`https://repo.repsy.io/nilsen84/maven`); deps `minecraft com.mojang:minecraft:1.21.1`, `yarn 1.21.1+build.3:v2`, `loader 0.16.9`, `fabric-api 0.102.1+1.21.1`, ultralight-bindings `implementation`+`include` (generic jar) and `runtimeOnly`+`include` (windows-x64 classifier). `processResources` expands `${version}` into `fabric.mod.json`. Java 21 source/target/release with `withSourcesJar()`.
- `gradle.properties`: `org.gradle.jvmargs=-Xmx2G`, `org.gradle.configuration-cache=false`, `mod_version=0.1.0`, `maven_group=com.batclient`, `archives_base_name=bat-client-mod`.
- `settings.gradle`: pluginManagement = Fabric maven + mavenCentral + gradlePluginPortal; `rootProject.name='bat-client-mod'`.
- wrapper: `gradle-9.6.1-bin.zip`, `validateDistributionUrl=true`.
- `fabric.mod.json`: id `batclient`, `environment:"client"`, entrypoint `com.batclient.BatClientMod`, depends `fabricloader>=0.16.9`, `minecraft>=1.21`, `java>=21`, `fabric-key-binding-api-v1`, `fabric-rendering-v1`, `fabric-lifecycle-events-v1` (all `*`). Name "Bat Client", license MIT.
- The web UI ships as **file:// from disk** (`<gameDir>/batclient-web`), NOT as a jar-internal URL — that's why `WebAssetManager` exists; Ultralight's file path resolution is normalized/confined by `BatWebFileSystem.resolve()` (handles `file:///C:/...`, raw paths, strips a leading slash before a drive letter, resolve-relative-to-root, then `normalize().startsWith(webRoot)` confinement).

Not started.

## 2026-09-30 (2nd session) — native temp-dir leak fix + doc expansion

- User flagged that every launch creates a new `%TEMP%\ultralight-internal-natives-*` dir and worried about the user experience ("Broo it will take too much space of user it will ruin user expreicne").
- Audited `UltralightLoader.load()` bytecode (javap): confirmed `Files.createTempDirectory("ultralight-internal-natives-")` per run, read natives from classpath (`META-INF/jars` via loom include), no cleanup.
- Fix landed in `WebController.java`: `cleanStaleNatives()` (static) sweeps `%TEMP%` for `ultralight-internal-natives-*` and recursively deletes each; called at the top of `loadNatives()` (before `UltralightLoader.INSTANCE.load()`) and again from `BatClientMod` CLIENT_STOPPING (current run's own dir is DLL-locked at that point, so it survives until the next launch's sweep — steady state = one dir).
- Compile gotcha hit: javac treated `%TEMP%\ultralight` in a Javadoc as `\u` unicode escape → "illegal unicode escape". Reworded the comment to avoid backslash-u.
- Verified live (test29): planted `ultralight-internal-natives-fake1..3` → launch → log shows `Cleaned stale Ultralight natives dir: ...fake1/2/3` then `Ultralight natives loaded` → fakes gone, exactly one real dir remains. User: "It is working nowww yhhh!!".
- Disk math answered for the user: at most one ~45 MB dir at any time; the per-launch copy still happens but never accumulates; NOT a network download (DLLs ship inside the mod jar).

## 2026-10-04 sessions — Feather pause screen + resource-pack browser + feedback fixes (WEB_VERSION 15 → 18)

Round 1 built the feature set; round 2 (same day) fixed the user's four feedback items. Both rounds shipped together as WEB_VERSION **18** (current).

### Feature build (round 1, WEB_VERSION 15→17)

- **`WebScreen.Mode` enum** `{MENU, OVERLAY, PAUSE}`: MENU = title takeover (index.html, ESC ignored), OVERLAY = Right Shift (overlay.html, world keeps running), PAUSE = ESC pause (pause.html, world pauses). `shouldPause()` = mode==PAUSE, `shouldCloseOnEsc()` = mode!=MENU. `init()` revives after `removed()` (rebuild window + `adoptWindow` + re-register).
- **Pause takeover**: `BatClientMod.tick` swaps vanilla `GameMenuScreen` for the PAUSE WebScreen in the same tick (before render → no vanilla flash). One-shot `preferOriginalPause` flag set by the page's "Original Pause Screen" action; reset when `currentScreen == null`.
- **`pause.html`**: 4-icon right rail (server list / screenshots / resource packs / original pause, `data-tip` tooltips, no chat), center stack (brand + Back to Game / Bat Client Settings / red STORE → website / Options / Save and Quit to Title), 5 bottom minis (Advancements / Statistics / Open World / Open to LAN [hidden unless `batIsSingleplayer`=="true"] / Minecraft Folder), `#shots-layer` screenshots browser (`batScreenshots` bridge = JSON of newest ≤64 PNGs in `<gameDir>/screenshots`; click → `openshot:` opens externally; "No Screenshots :(" empty state), full panel-layer copy, `tickClock` sun/moon + `yyyy/MM/dd hh:mm A` pill (15s interval).
- **`respacks.html`**: Feather-style Modrinth resource-pack browser — page-side `fetch` to `api.modrinth.com/v2/search` (CORS `*` works in Ultralight; 14 sidebar filters = 10 categories + 4 features, slugs verified via `/v2/tag/category?project_type=resourcepack`), search debounce 350ms, sort dropdown (relevance/downloads/updated), card grid (art = `featured_gallery || gallery[0] || icon_url`), hover INSTALL → `installrespack:<id>` → Java background thread (latest version → .zip → sanitized filename → `client.getResourcePackDir()`) → `queueScript` → `window.__rpInstallDone(ok,msg)` toast.
- **Java bridges**: `batScreenshots`, `batIsSingleplayer`, prefix handlers `openshot:` (name regex-confined) + `installrespack:` (project-id regex-confined). `handleAction` gained: savequit/serverlist/resourcepacks(PackScreen)/originalpause/advancements/statistics/opentolan/openworlds/mcfolder/openshotsfolder/settings (PAUSE → parent = this so Done returns to pause).
- **`WebController.queueScript()`**: thread-safe script queue drained at the top of `updateAndRender()` (views are thread-affine — never `evaluateScript` from background threads).

### Feedback fixes (round 2 — user screenshots)

1. **"when pause menu is open and we press escape it doesn't close it"** — root cause: Java forwarded ESC to the page (`window.onKey`) and the page's `keydown` ladder never fired (Ultralight doesn't deliver ESC to page JS while the MC window handles input). Fix: ESC ladder moved into `WebScreen.keyPressed` (PAUSE mode): `evaluateScript` returns `1` (shots layer open → run `closeShots()`), `2` (`respacks-page` → `loadUrl(pause.html)`), else `close()` → world. Result is quote-stripped (`replace("\"","")`) because bindings may serialize strings with quotes; unexpected values fall through to `close()`. `app.js` keydown handler kept as harmless fallback (Java no longer forwards ESC in PAUSE, so it can't double-fire).
2. **"pause screen doesn't have the option to go back to bat client screen"** (from the vanilla Original Pause) — new **`OriginalPauseScreen.java`**: composes vanilla `GameMenuScreen` as a **delegate** (no mixins): `init()` calls `delegate.init(client,w,h)` then adds a Feather-style grey **"Bat Client Screen"** button top-right (`width-170, 6, 164×20`, label same as Feather's "Feather Pause Screen"); `render` = `delegate.render(...)` then `super.render(...)`; **`renderBackground` overridden no-op** because `Screen.render` = `renderBackground` + drawables loop (and `Screen.drawables` is **private** — can't iterate it directly); input forwarding = `super` first, then delegate; ESC → `close()` → world (flag resets). `BatClientMod.tick` now wraps any `GameMenuScreen` when `preferOriginalPause` (skip if already the wrapper); `showPauseScreen` made `public static`; flag also resets when `client.world == null` (quit-to-title with stale flag). Vanilla buttons inside the delegate that swap screens work unchanged; on return to the vanilla menu the tick re-wraps it.
3. **"quit game just make the screen black"** — root cause: our `savequit` called only `client.disconnect()` = `disconnect(ProgressScreen(closeAfterFinished=true), false)`; when the world save finishes, `ProgressScreen.render` does `done && closeAfterFinished → setScreen(null)` and **nothing ever shows a TitleScreen** → black forever. Fix = exact vanilla `GameMenuScreen.disconnect()` sequence: `world.disconnect()`; singleplayer → `client.disconnect(new MessageScreen(Text.translatable("menu.savingLevel")))` else `client.disconnect()`; then `setScreen(new TitleScreen())` (sp) / `RealmsMainScreen(titleScreen)` (Realms) / `MultiplayerScreen(titleScreen)` (mp). Imports: `MessageScreen`, `ServerInfo` (`net.minecraft.client.network`), `RealmsMainScreen` (**`net.minecraft.client.realms.gui.screen`** — NOT `gui.screen.world`). Title takeover tick then swaps TitleScreen → Bat main menu.
4. **"resource pack aren't the same as I gave the ss"** — root cause found with the smoke harness: **`.rp-grid` had `overflow-y:auto` on the grid element itself**; this Ultralight WebKit **mis-sizes auto grid rows to ~56px when the grid is its own scroller** (`cardScrollH=335` vs `cardClientH=54`) → cards rendered as 56px slices: banner image top only, no title/desc/chips (matches the user's "image-only strips" screenshot). Proof: live probe `g.style.overflow='visible'` → rows instantly became `336.75px`. Fix: new **`.rp-scroll` wrapper** (`flex:1; min-height:0; overflow-y:auto`) is the scroller; `.rp-grid` keeps `display:grid` but **never overflow**. Verified: `row0=333.84`, `cardH=334 == cardScrollH=332`, cards render full title/author/downloads/desc/chips.
   Also aligned the page to Feather's reference layout: `.rp-head` `left/right: 15.7%` (aligned with panel edges, not window corners) + `padding-top: 14vh`; `.rp-panel` floating `top: 21vh; bottom: 16vh; left/right: 15.7%` (measured `panelTop=218, panelBottom=873, left=263, right=1411` vs Feather's 244/873/266/1403 — near-identical).
5. **"too bluish"** (pause) — `.pause-page .overlay-dim` `0.55 → 0.76` (alpha probe 194; Feather reads near-black — the bluish cast was the world showing through); `.respacks-page .overlay-dim` → `0.40` (Feather's respacks bg is a bright blurred world); neutralized blue-leaning grays: `.pause-btn` `#121317 → #161616` (hover `#242424`), `.pause-tool` `rgba(16,17,21,.88) → rgba(18,18,18,.88)` (hover `#343434`), `.pause-mini` `rgba(13,14,17,.78) → rgba(15,15,15,.78)` (hover `#1e1e1e`).

- `gradlew compileJava --offline` → EXIT=0 (wrapper + savequit + ESC ladder compile against yarn 1.21.1).
- `PageSmokeTest` **all 4 modes PASS**: menu, overlay, pause (`pauseProbe=5|4|5|STORE|Back to Game|esc`, `shotsProbe=open|No Screenshots :(`, `cornerAlpha=194`), respacks (`rpProbe=cards:|14|24|in|RESOURCE PACKS`, geom `rows=333.84 cardH=334 headLeft=263 panelTop=218 panelBottom=873`, `cornerAlpha=102`).
- Harness extensions: pause/respacks render at the user's real window size **1674×1039**; PNG dump of the surface to `%TEMP%\opencode\ultra-test\<mode>-shot.png` (BufferedImage via `lockPixels`, BGRA→ARGB) — eyeballed: respacks now matches Feather's reference (floating panel, header above aligned to panel, full cards with text/chips).
- `runClient` restarted with `run\batclient-web\version.txt` deleted → v18 re-extraction to verify on boot (`version.txt` must read `18`).
- **Still needs live in-game confirmation**: `OriginalPauseScreen` button click-through, ESC ladder steps, savequit → title, card images loading over network (they exist in DOM; smoke runs without gallery images).

### Tooling (reusable)

- Smoke: `PageSmokeTest <webDir> <page> <menu|overlay|pause|respacks>`; classpath = bindings + natives + kotlin-stdlib jars (see "Key technical references"); exit 0xC0000409 after `PAGE_SMOKE_PASS` is the known teardown quirk — judge by the verdict line only.
- `javap` on the yarn-mapped jar is the source of truth for MC APIs (this session: `MinecraftClient.disconnect()` overloads — no-arg = `disconnect(new ProgressScreen(true), false)`; `Screen.render` = background + drawables; `Screen.drawables` is private).

## 2026-10-04/05 session (3rd) — respack-install fixes, de-redding, infinite scroll, first-menu flash groundwork (WEB_VERSION 18 → 20)

Round 3 followed the WEB_VERSION 18 release. All of this section is the state NOT covered by the 15→18 section above. **Current live web asset version = `20`** (supersedes every "current" WEB_VERSION claim above; `run\batclient-web\version.txt` verified = 20).

### A. Resource-pack install — root causes found & FIXED (user: "Fixed")

The install *transport* worked all along (Java thread fetched/downloaded/unzipped fine); failures were selection/validation, three separate bugs:

1. **Version selection picked the wrong Modrinth version.** Worker took `versionArray[0]` = newest release with no MC-version check → e.g. FreshAnimations **v1.10.5** (only `game_versions` = 26.x, `min_format:84`) installed against 1.21.1 → MC rejects metadata with `No key pack_format` → pack silently hidden from the Available list (user saw "installed" but nothing usable). **Fix in `WebScreen.startRespackInstall`**: pass 1 = first version whose `game_versions` array **contains the running MC version**; pass 2 (fallback) = only versions whose `game_versions` is **empty** (mods like toadew's `slufHzC2` ship no version metadata but do work); else fail with toast **"No version of this pack supports Minecraft 1.21.1."** (dynamic version). Helper added: `jsonArrayContains(JsonArray arr, String v)`. Log line: `[respacks] install <id> for MC <ver>` → `[respacks] installed <file> v<ver> (<bytes>)` or `[respacks] install failed: <msg>` (failures logged in `finishRespackInstall` — previously silent).
   - Verified facts: FreshAnimations **v1.10.4 supports 1.21.1** (v1.10.5 does not); `Fresh Moves` (`slufHzC2`) = 1.21.3/1.21.4 only → correctly shows the "no version" toast.
2. **`MinecraftClient.getGameVersion()` returns `"Fabric"` in dev runs** — loom passes `--version Fabric` (the *loader* name), not the MC version → every pack looked incompatible ("every pack says No version…"). **Fix**: use `net.minecraft.SharedConstants.VERSION_NAME` (fallback `client.getGameVersion()` if null/empty). Same source now drives both install selection and the error-message version.
3. **Filename regex rejected spaces.** Old guard `[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\.zip` rejected legit Modrinth filenames like `Fancy Crops v1.3.zip` / `Bare Bones 1.21.11.zip` → "installing failed for some resource packs". **Fix = sanitization instead of rejection**: take the basename only (strip `\`/`/`), remove `[<>:"|?*\x00-\x1F]`, strip leading/trailing dots/spaces (spaces allowed), reject only if the result doesn't end in `.zip` → error `Modrinth sent an unusable file name.`

User feedback sequence driving these: "resource pack problem isn't fixed" (stale broken v1.10.5 zip from the earlier bug was still sitting in `run\resourcepacks`) → "installing failed for some resource packs" (spaces) → "every pack says No version…" (`getGameVersion()=="Fabric"`) → confirmed Fixed. In-game proof: log shows `file/FreshAnimations_v1.10.4.zip` + `file/SodiumTranslations.zip` loaded as enabled packs on 1.21.1.

### B. Infinite scroll (respacks page) — "IF I scroll more there should be more resourcepacks"

- `RP_STATE.offset` added; `rpBuildUrl(offset)` appends `&offset=<n>`; `rpFetch(append)` (Modrinth search honors `offset`), `rpRender(hits, append)`, `rpSearch()` resets `offset=0` + clears `loading`, `rpMore()` = next-page fetch, `rpMaybeFill()` = viewport chain-fill (keeps fetching while the `.rp-scroll` content doesn't fill the box).
- Scroll listener on **`#rp-scroll`** (id added to the wrapper div — NOT on `window`): fires `rpMore()` when `scrollHeight - scrollTop - clientHeight < 400`.
- `rpLoading` + `rpTotal` guards prevent parallel/duplicate fetches; `rpTotal` from `search_result.count`.
- Drove **WEB_VERSION 18 → 19**.

### C. De-redding — "why resourcepack screen have red" (user: white + dark black only)

- `.rp-red` (INSTALL/SUBSCRIBE-class buttons) → **white gradient** `#ffffff → #e6e7ea`, border `rgba(0,0,0,0.35)`, text `#101014`; `.rp-red:hover` → `filter: brightness(0.92)` (no hue change).
- `.shots-x` (screenshots close) → white border/text (`rgba(255,255,255,.08)` bg, `.55` border, `#fff` color; hover `.20` bg).
- `.rp-toast-err` → white bg + black text.
- STORE button **keeps** its red Feather gradient (matches the approved screenshot design; the "red" complaint was the resourcepack screen).
- Drove **WEB_VERSION 19 → 20**; verified extracted `run\batclient-web\style.css` contains no `#ef4444`, `version.txt` = 20.

### D. First-menu flash — ANALYSIS + partial groundwork (the open task)

User: *"Fixed but there is a problem when open a first time menu after opening game it opens another menu for few milli seconds or seconds"* — on the first menu open after launching the game, a vanilla menu (title/pause) flashes for ms/seconds before the web UI replaces it. Subsequent opens are fine.

**Boot timeline (19:00 run, `run\logs\latest.log`)**: natives loaded `19:00:43` → renderer created `19:00:45` → `index.html` loaded + `[bat] init ok` + first upload `19:00:53` — an ~8 s gap between renderer creation and page load, during which the vanilla title screen owns the frame.

**javap frame-order facts** (dump = `%TEMP%\opencode\mcclient.txt`, 500416 B / 8167 lines; `javap -c -p` of `MinecraftClient` from the yarn-mapped jar):
- MC 1.21.1 has **no `runTick`**; loop methods are `run()`, `render(boolean)`, `tick()`, private `handleInputEvents()`.
- `handleInputEvents()` is invoked at ~4% into `tick()` (dump: header 4447, end 8165, invoke 4604) → Fabric's END_CLIENT_TICK tail hook runs after it → the `GameMenuScreen` built inside that same `tick()` is swapped by our takeover **before any render** → vanilla GameMenu should never get a frame from that path.
- `openGameMenu(boolean)` (dump line 4039) constructs `GameMenuScreen` (4059/4073) but has **zero callers inside `MinecraftClient`** — the caller class was never identified (jar-wide string scan script `C:\Users\Noor-ulIslam\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\find-openGameMenu.ps1` timed out twice; correct path uses `Noor-ul-Islam`). Unresolved: a pause opened from outside `tick()` could render one vanilla frame.
- Mapped jar: `C:\Users\Noor-ul-Islam\.gradle\caches\fabric-loom\minecraftMaven\net\minecraft\minecraft-merged\1.21.1-net.fabricmc.yarn.1_21_1.1.21.1+build.3-v2\minecraft-merged-1.21.1-...jar`.

**Groundwork already compiled into the tree (with two known defects — NOT yet verified/finished):**
1. `WebWindow`: `volatile boolean painted` + `hasPainted()`. ⚠ **Defect**: `painted = true` is currently set in `draw()` after `drawWithGlobalProgram` — wrong; it must be set in `renderAndUpload()` after the first *successful texture upload*. As placed, the first drawn frame (empty texture) marks painted → `WebScreen.render()` stops drawing the backdrop too early.
2. `WebScreen.render()`: backdrop fill before `updateAndRender` while `!window.hasPainted()` — `0xFF000000` (opaque black) for `Mode.MENU`, `0x8C000000` (67 % dim) otherwise, sized `this.width/this.height`.
3. Warm pause reuse: `BatClientMod.cachedPauseScreen` + `showPauseScreen` reuse branch (`cachedPauseScreen.window() != null` → `WebController.adoptWindow(screen.window())` instead of `createScreen`) + `clearCachedPause()`. ⚠ **Defect**: (a) `tick()` invalidation `client.world == null || client.currentScreen == null || !(currentScreen instanceof WebScreen)` fires `currentScreen == null` on *resume* → cache cleared every pause; (b) `WebScreen.removed()` PAUSE branch sets `window = null` → `screen.window()` is null → reuse branch can never trigger. Net effect confirmed in log: `Loading pause.html` on **every** ESC.
4. `WebScreen.removed()` PAUSE branch skips `closeWindow()` (keeps the Ultralight view alive for reuse) — correct intent, undermined by the `window = null` above.

**Planned fix (reasoned, not yet coded):** (1) move `painted=true` into `renderAndUpload()` after upload; (2) cache invalidation = `client.world == null` only (drop the `currentScreen == null` clause); (3) `removed()` PAUSE branch must NOT null the `window` field; (4) boot-time MENU preload — create the MENU WebScreen + load `index.html` during splash and pump `renderer.update()/render()` **without** `renderAndUpload()` while `SplashOverlay` is up (NVIDIA nvoglv64 crashes if the GL upload races the splash; software-side update/render is safe) so the takeover swaps in a fully-loaded page with zero gap; (5) `showMainMenu` reuse of a cached menu screen (mirror of the pause cache).

### E. File deletions (user directive)

User: delete unnecessary project stuff, don't touch unrecognized/other-project files. Deleted: `bat-client-mod/build/`, `run\resourcepacks\FreshAnimations_v1.10.5.zip` (broken zip from bug A1), `run\logs\latest.log`, `run\runclient.out`. Everything else left alone.

### F. Tooling/gotchas accumulated this round (supersede older instructions)

- **PowerShell has no `grep`/`tail`/`head`/`&&`**: use `Select-String`, `Select-Object -Last N`, and `cmd /c "cd /d <path> && gradlew.bat ..."`.
- **Never combine** `taskkill`/`Stop-Process java` or `gradlew --stop` with other commands in one shell call → tool dies with `ChildProcess.kill`. Run them as the sole command.
- **Gradle UP-TO-DATE is unreliable** right after edits (stale daemon FS-watch): `compileJava`/`jar` may say UP-TO-DATE while classes *did* recompile (or vice versa) — verify by `ReadAllText` on the output `.class` searching for a new-code string marker before restarting the game.
- **runClient executes `build\classes\java\main`, not the jar** — class-verify that tree.
- **runClient start pattern**: `Start-Process cmd -ArgumentList "/c cd /d C:\Users\Noor-ul-Islam\Desktop\Raven-Client\bat-client-mod && gradlew.bat runClient --offline" -WindowStyle Hidden`, then poll `run\logs\latest.log` for `[bat] init ok`. (Correct repo path: `Noor-ul-Islam`, with the hyphen.)
- **Never edit a file without reading it first** — user: "stop making Could not find oldString".
- User pace complaint: "why are too slow these days… 15 minutes thinking and only 2 commands" — batch tool calls, less deliberation before acting.

## 2026-10-07 session (4th) — first-menu flash fix implemented + boot-verified

User: *"Fixed but there is a problem when open a first time menu after opening game it opens another menu for few milli seconds or seconds"* — vanilla title flashed briefly at boot before the web menu took over. All defects listed in section D above are now fixed.

### Root causes

1. **Takeover waited for `getOverlay() == null`** → vanilla TitleScreen kept rendering through the whole splash fade-out; swap only happened at splash end — that fade was the visible "another menu" flash.
2. **`painted` flag set in `draw()`** (after drawing the never-uploaded texture) instead of after the first successful GL upload → backdrop dropped a frame early.
3. **`draw()` ran even while `!hasPainted()`** → the undefined (never-uploaded) texture quad was drawn over the backdrop.
4. **Warm pause cache was dead (two bugs):** tick invalidated `cachedPauseScreen` whenever `currentScreen == null` (exactly the state right after closing the pause screen), AND `removed()`/`close()` nulled `window` / called `closeWindow()` for PAUSE → every ESC reloaded `pause.html`.
5. **`WebController.createScreen()` destroyed a live cached view without clearing the cache** → a warm-reuse could adopt an already-closed window.

### Fixes (compiled + boot-verified, this session)

- `BatClientMod.tick` main-menu takeover: **dropped `client.getOverlay() == null`** — swaps TitleScreen → WebScreen as soon as `isReady()`, even mid-splash; the web screen's solid backdrop covers the vanilla title for the rest of the splash.
- `WebScreen.render`: **gated `updateAndRender()` on `client.getOverlay() == null`** — Ultralight pump / first GL upload still never run while the splash draws (preserves the NVIDIA nvoglv64 constraint: the crash was the *upload* racing the splash, not the screen swap). Structure now: backdrop fill while `!window.hasPainted()`, `window.draw()` ONLY when painted (never draw the undefined texture).
- `WebWindow`: `painted = true` moved from `draw()` into `renderAndUpload()` right after `surface.clearDirtyBounds()` (first successful `glTexSubImage2D`).
- `WebScreen.close()`: teardown block wrapped in `if (mode != Mode.PAUSE)` (safety net only); `removed()` PAUSE branch keeps BOTH the view and the `window` field (no more `window = null`) → `screen.window() != null` holds for reuse.
- `WebScreen.init()`: re-registers `adoptWindow` + `setActiveScreen` on EVERY activation (was only in the `window == null` rebuild branch — a kept window returning from Options/PackScreen never re-registered).
- `BatClientMod.tick` cache invalidation: **`client.world == null` only** (was `world==null || currentScreen==null || !(currentScreen instanceof WebScreen)`).
- `WebController.createScreen()`: after `closeWindow()` calls `BatClientMod.clearCachedPause()` (now `public`) — single choke point: any fresh screen creation invalidates the cache whose view was just destroyed.
- NOT done from the plan: boot-time MENU *preload* + `showMainMenu` warm cache (item 4/5 of the plan) — the early swap + backdrop made them unnecessary; the page still loads (file://, ~0.1 s) right after the splash clears, behind a solid black backdrop, so no vanilla menu can flash.

### Boot verification (19:35 run, `run\logs\latest.log`)

- `19:35:25` renderer created → `19:35:29` `Loading .../index.html` (swap happened DURING splash — old code logged this at splash end) → `19:35:31` first Ultralight pump (`Initializing Thread Pool` = first `renderer.update()`) = gate released when overlay cleared → `[bat] init ok` + `upload first-frame alpha0=255` + `bridge probe: function|...|function` same second.
- The 2 s gap between loadUrl (29) and first pump (31) IS the gate working: swap during splash, upload after splash. No `hs_err_pid*.log`, no batclient ERRORs. Compile EXIT=0; class markers verified in `build\classes\java\main` (`WebScreen.class` contains `getOverlay`, `WebController.class` contains `clearCachedPause`) because gradle UP-TO-DATE is unreliable.

## 2026-10-07 session (5th) — white STORE, screenshot viewer, ESC-flash root cause fixed (WEB_VERSION 20 → 21)

User (with pause-screen screenshot): *"make store button white and add a in-game screenshot viewer like can view it and when spam esc there is a milli second of normal minecraft pause menu can you fix it?"* — three changes, all compiled + booted live (v21 extracted).

### 1. STORE button white

- `style.css` `.pause-btn-store`: red Feather gradient `(#f0454b→#b71f27)` → white gradient `linear-gradient(135deg, #ffffff, #e6e7ea)`, `color:#101014`, `border-color: rgba(0,0,0,.35)`; hover = same white + `filter:brightness(0.92)` + white glow shadow (matches the `.rp-red` de-redding style from session 3).
- `pause.html`: STORE button's bag SVG `stroke="#fff"` → `stroke="#101014"` (icon must be dark on the white button).

### 2. In-page screenshot viewer (lightbox) — thumb click now OPENS the viewer, not external

- Existing plumbing reused: `BatWebFileSystem.resolve()` ALREADY has the `screenshots` alias (`<webroot>/screenshots/<name>` → `<gameDir>/screenshots/<name>`, single-component name, normalized, inside gameDir) — so `img.src = "screenshots/" + name` works for both thumbs and the viewer; `batScreenshots` JSON bridge + `openshot:` action unchanged.
- `pause.html`: new `#shot-viewer` overlay after `#shots-layer`: `.shot-viewer` (fixed, `z-index:6`, `rgba(0,0,0,.88)` bg) + `.shot-viewer-x` close + two `.shot-nav` prev/next circular buttons (`‹` `›`) + `.shot-viewer-stage` (`#shot-view-img`, max 82vw×74vh, `object-fit:contain`) + `.shot-viewer-bar` (`#shot-viewer-name`, `#shot-viewer-count` = "i / n", "Open Externally" `.pause-mini` button → `shotopenextern` → `callBat("openshot:"+current)`).
- `style.css`: viewer CSS block after `.shots-foot` (all white/black theme).
- `app.js`: `window.__shotNames`/`__shotIndex` state; `openShotView(i)` (wrap-around index, sets img/name/count, unhides), `closeShotView()`, `shotViewOpen()`, `stepShot(±1)`; `openShots()` stores names and thumb click → `openShotView(i)` (was `openshot:`); new switch cases `shotviewerclose`/`shotprev`/`shotnext`/`shotopenextern`; `closeShots()` also closes the viewer; JS keydown fallback ladder gained a viewer-first step.
- `WebScreen.keyPressed` Java ESC ladder (authoritative): query script now returns **3 = viewer open → `closeShotView()`**, 1 = shots layer, 2 = respacks, 0 = close to world.

### 3. Spam-ESC millisecond vanilla pause flash — ROOT CAUSE FOUND + FIXED

- **Root cause (jar scan)**: `openGameMenu` is referenced by **`net/minecraft/client/Keyboard.class`** (and `GameRenderer.class`) — the pause key is handled in the **GLFW key callback**, which can fire OUTSIDE `MinecraftClient.tick()` (between END_CLIENT_TICK and the frame render) → the vanilla `GameMenuScreen` got created after our tick handler had already run → it rendered **one frame** before the next tick's takeover swapped it. (The earlier javap note "zero callers in MinecraftClient" was right — the caller is Keyboard.)
- **Fix: `ScreenEvents.BEFORE_INIT`** (fabric-screen-api-v1) registered in `BatClientMod.onInitializeClient`: fires inside `setScreen → Screen.init` no matter when/where the GameMenuScreen was created → swap to the web pause (or wrap in `OriginalPauseScreen` when `preferOriginalPause`) **before it can ever render**. Guards: `screen instanceof GameMenuScreen && !(screen instanceof OriginalPauseScreen)`, `client.world != null`, `isReady()`, and `!(client.currentScreen instanceof OriginalPauseScreen)` (recursion guard — OriginalPauseScreen re-inits its vanilla delegate, which would re-fire BEFORE_INIT for the same GameMenuScreen).
- **Reentrancy verified via javap** (`MinecraftClient.setScreen`, dump line 2284): after `screen.init(...)` returns it only does `skipGameRender = false` + `updateWindowTitle()` — safe when our hook re-enters `setScreen` from inside `init`. Bytecode order: `currentScreen.removed()` → assign `currentScreen` → `onDisplayed` → `BufferRenderer.reset` → `mouse.unlockCursor` → `KeyBinding.unpressAll` → `screen.init(...)` **[HOOK FIRES HERE]** → done.
- END_CLIENT_TICK takeover kept as harmless fallback (sees WebScreen → no-ops).
- `fabric.mod.json` `depends` gained `"fabric-screen-api-v1": "*"`.

### Build/verify this round

- `gradlew compileJava --offline` EXIT=0; class markers verified in `build\classes\java\main`: `BatClientMod.class` contains `ScreenEvents`, `WebScreen.class` contains `shot-viewer` (compiled 20:19:23 — gradle UP-TO-DATE unreliable, always string-check).
- `WEB_VERSION "20" → "21"`; runClient restarted → `run\batclient-web\version.txt` = **21**, re-extract logged 20:23:34; extracted style.css has `.shot-viewer` + white store gradient + NO `#f0454b`; pause.html has `shot-viewer`; boot clean (`[bat] init ok`, no ERROR).
- Shell quirk recurred: `ChildProcess.kill` killed the tool on a `compileJava` invocation — re-running the SAME command alone succeeded. Keep `taskkill` and any gradle command in separate tool calls.
