## 2026-10-07 session (6th) � Feather-style WORKING modules: research + architecture + API verification (WEB_VERSION 21 -> 22, functional round in progress)

User directive: *"make them actually work and it isn't the same I sent you the screenshots and search about feather client on web and make modules like that and etc all the settings make them functional and Hud editor should work and can resize the modules on screen and after closing settings there is no modules on screen."*

### Root problem identified

The mod-menu "modules" are web widgets (`.hud-widget` divs) that only exist while the overlay page is open. Closing settings/overlay = nothing on screen (exactly the user's complaint), and none of the settings actually change game behavior. Feather's model: modules are **client-rendered HUD elements + tick-driven client mods**, with a drag/resize editor. So the fix is Java-side rendering driven by the same config the web editor edits.

### Architecture decisions (locked this session)

1. **Java HUD renderer** on Fabric `HudRenderCallback` (fabric-rendering-v1 is already a dependency) renders all HUD modules every frame from config. Renders when `world != null && !options.hudHidden && !BatClientMod.isHudEditorActive()` (editor active = web widgets show instead, crisp; otherwise in-game Java HUD).
2. **Config = single source of truth**: `config/batclient-hud.json` via new `com.batclient.HudConfig` (Gson; `snapshot()/json()/update()/module/enabled/posX/posY/scale/str/bool/setNumber`). Web reads/writes it via NEW bindings `batGetHudConfig` / `batSaveHudConfig` (still to be added to `WebScreen.bindBridge`); localStorage only as fallback. Web switches its store from `prefGet/prefSet("bat.mod.*"...)` to the CFG object (NOT yet done in app.js).
3. **Editor suppression flag**: `hudedit:on` / `hudedit:off` batActions (from `toggleHudEditor`) set `BatClientMod.setHudEditorActive(...)`, reset false in `WebScreen.removed()` for OVERLAY (WebScreen.java:564 already has `evaluateScript("toggleHudEditor(false)")` � verify placement when editing).
4. **Coordinate mapping insight**: Ultralight view is sized in **framebuffer pixels** (CSS 1px = 1 framebuffer px); MC Screen mouse coords are GUI-scaled and multiplied by `xScale = framebuffer/scaledWidth` (`WebWindow.java:253/268`) before firing into the view. Percent positions are space-agnostic ? web stores x/y as % of viewport; Java computes % of `getScaledWidth()/getScaledHeight()` ? same screen spot. Do NOT store pixel values.
5. **ESC ladder** (`WebScreen.keyPressed`, OVERLAY branch): mod-settings(5)?closeModSettings, panel(4)?closePanel, hud-edit?toggleHudEditor(false) (editor off first, not close), else close. Implemented in the v22 round.
6. **Script tags are at body end** in all 3 pages (overlay:162, pause:209, index:119) ? DOM ready when app.js runs; `getElementById("hud-layer")` at init is safe.

### Module set (12) + defaults � MUST be identical in Java and app.js

HUD (Java-rendered): `fps` T (2,6), `coords` T (2,16), `direction` T (2,26), `speed` F (2,36), `ping` F (2,46), `cps` F (2,56), `keystrokes` T (3,62), `potions` T (64,6), `armor` F (86,6).
Client (tick-driven): `zoom` (KeyBinding C, FOV save/restore, settings level 2-10 + smooth), `fullbright` (injects NIGHT_VISION StatusEffect, no settings -> hide gear), `togglesprint` (auto-sprint while forward held, no settings -> hide gear). zoom/fullbright/togglesprint default ON.

Settings schemas (all must be functional; colors: White/Gray/Green/Yellow/Aqua/Black � no red/purple):
- common: `scale` (0.5-3), `shadow`, `color`
- fps: +nothing more. coords: +`labels`. direction: +`axis` ("South (+Z)" vs "South"). speed: +`hideStill`. ping/cps: common only.
- keystrokes: `background`, `border`, `showCps`, `pressedColor` (replaces the old non-functional Direction/Animation choice rows). potions: `format` Shorthand("1:24")/Full("1m 24s") � icons NOT implementable (no atlas access) -> drop. armor: +`numbers`.
- zoom: `level` slider 2-10, `smooth` toggle.

### CPS tracking plan

`ClientMods.tick`: edge-detect `attackKey.isPressed()`/`useKey.isPressed()` (only when `currentScreen == null`), push `System.currentTimeMillis()` into ArrayDeques, prune >1000ms, `lmbCps()/rmbCps()` return size. Keystrokes + cps modules read these.

### Zoom plan (no mixins)

Save `options.getFov().getValue()` on zoom start; while held target = `max(1, saved/level)`, lerp 0.35/tick when `smooth`, else direct set; restore savedFov on release/disabled/world-null. Held = `zoomKey.isPressed() && currentScreen == null`.

**Mixin infra (new — the mod had NO mixins until now, despite AGENTS.md layout saying "Mixins")**:
- NEW `src/main/resources/batclient.mixins.json`: package `com.batclient.mixin`, compatibilityLevel JAVA_21, refmap `batclient-refmap.json`, client: `["ParticleThrottleMixin"]`, `defaultRequire: 1`.
- `fabric.mod.json` gained `"mixins": ["batclient.mixins.json"]`.
- Loom 1.17.21 applies the mixin AP automatically (build.gradle untouched); loader jar (with `org.spongepowered.asm`) was already on the compile classpath → offline compile expected to work (was about to verify when notes were requested).

**NEW `com/batclient/mixin/ParticleThrottleMixin.java`**: `@Mixin(ParticleManager.class)` (class `net.minecraft.client.particle.ParticleManager`), `@Inject(method = "addParticle(Lnet/minecraft/particle/ParticleEffect;DDDDDD)Lnet/minecraft/client/particle/Particle;", at = HEAD, cancellable)` → `if (ClientMods.dropParticle()) ci.setReturnValue(null);` (non-void method → `setReturnValue`, not bare `cancel()`).

**`ClientMods.java` edits**:
- Import `net.minecraft.client.option.Perspective`.
- New fields: `perspectiveKey`, `private static volatile int particlePct = 50`.
- `init()` registers second KeyBinding `key.batclient.perspective` (KEYSYM **GLFW_KEY_P**, category `category.batclient.title`).
- `tick()`: `particlePct = particlePctFrom(cfg)` (once per tick) + `perspective(client, enabledDef(cfg, "perspective", true))`.
- `perspective()`: guard `enabled && currentScreen==null && key!=null`; `while (perspectiveKey.wasPressed()) client.options.setPerspective(client.options.getPerspective().next())`.
- `particlePctFrom(cfg)`: module `particlereduce` disabled → 0; `amount` slider (def 50) clamped 0..100.
- `public static boolean dropParticle()`: reads ONLY the volatile int (pct<=0 → never, pct>=100 → always, else `ThreadLocalRandom.nextInt(100) < pct`) — per-spawn config parsing was the reason for the tick cache.

**Other files**: `en_us.json` + `key.batclient.perspective: "Perspective Toggle"`; `WebAssetManager.WEB_VERSION "23" → "24"`; `node --check app.js` → **APPJS_OK**.

**Why particle reduction got a mixin and not reflection**: `MinecraftClient.particleManager` is `public final` (direct access remaps fine) but `ParticleManager.particles` (the map) is private — yarn-named reflective field access **breaks in production** (intermediary mappings), while `@Shadow`/`@Inject` are remapped by the mixin AP. Spawn-throttle via `addParticle` HEAD avoids touching private state entirely. `Particle.markDead()`/`isAlive()` exist if a kill-based approach is ever needed; `ParticleManager.MAX_PARTICLE_COUNT` is `private static final int` + `public void tick()`.

## 2026-10-09 session (7th, round 3) - ONE renderer: editor shows the REAL Java HUD + keystrokes single-layer fix (WEB_VERSION 26 -> 27)

User feedback (1 screenshot of in-game keystrokes): "why there is so much layer of black and still you are using web hud modules in hud editor and in real you are using java one use 1 in both ... or use web one instead of java".

**Decision: unified on the JAVA renderer (one renderer in both places).** Reason: Java already has ALL live game data (real armor item icons, live key presses, CPS, coords, ping, potion timers, target HP) — the web-everywhere path would need a per-tick Java->JS state bridge (queueScript evaluateScript feed), would degrade armor/durability item icons to placeholder squares, and would require a second always-on Ultralight view + full-window texture upload every frame (perf risk on the user's Kepler machine). The editor instead now shows the SAME Java pixels underneath.

### How the single renderer works now

- `HudRenderer.render` no longer checks `BatClientMod.isHudEditorActive()` (line removed) — it draws whenever `world != null && !hudHidden`, EVEN while the overlay/editor screen is open (vanilla InGameHud renders under Screens, so HudRenderCallback still fires — hearts show behind the pause menu proof).
- `style.css` round-3 block (appended at end, title `round 3: ONE renderer`):
  - `body.hud-edit .overlay-dim { background: rgba(0,0,0,0.12) }` (was 0.55) -> world + Java widgets clearly visible while editing.
  - `body.hud-edit .hud-wbox { background: transparent; border: 1px dashed rgba(77,163,255,.85); padding: 4px 6px; min-width: 0; min-height: 0 }` — the DOM box is now an OUTLINE only; padding 4/6 matches the Java plate's 6h/4v (**key unit fact: web CSS px == GUI-scaled units**, because `windowContentScaleCallback(scale = fbWidth/scaledWidth)` makes 1 CSS px = 1 GUI unit — so Java 6 GUI units = 6 CSS px, NOT 12).
  - `body.hud-edit .hud-wbox > .hp, > .keys { visibility: hidden }` — module content hidden but STILL LAYS OUT, so the dashed outline hugs the widget's would-be size and `getBoundingClientRect()` drag/resize math keeps working. Only chrome stays visible: gear, hide, label pill, 4 resize handles.
- Editor flow: overlay opens with `toggleHudEditor(true)` by default -> body.hud-edit -> dim .12 + outlines; drag/resize -> `saveModState` -> `batSaveHudConfig` -> `HudConfig.update` (in-memory `memory` object, no re-read) -> next frame `HudConfig.snapshot()` picks it up -> the Java widget MOVES LIVE under the cursor. `hudedit:on/off` flag is now informational only (kept for ESC ladder/future).
- Stale "Java suppresses itself" comments updated: app.js `toggleHudEditor`, WebScreen `hudedit` cases + `removed()`, BatClientMod field comment, HudRenderer class javadoc.

### Keystrokes "so much layer of black" fix (HudRenderer)

Old look = plate(78% black) + per-key 0x70000000 fill + white border + LMB/RMB boxes + space box = dark stacked on dark. New look = ONE dark layer:
- With `background:true`: plate only; `box()` no longer draws the dark fill or white border — pressed keys/mouse get a light flash instead; new helper `pressFlash(mod)` = `(colorName(pressedColor) & 0x00FFFFFF) | 0x55000000` (rounded, fillRounded r=3).
- Space bar with background: thin `ctx.drawBorder(..., 0x44FFFFFF)` outline when idle, pressFlash when pressed — never a second filled box.
- With `background:false` (standalone): classic per-key dark box + border preserved (old behavior).
- Wrong-indent plate block from round 2 also fixed while rewriting.

### Preview aligned to the new game geometry (style.css round-2 `.keys` rules edited in place)

`.keys b/i` -> 16x16 px cells, font 9px, `background: transparent; border: none` (mirrors game text-only keys); `.keys .km` -> 36x14 text-only; `.keys .ks` -> 50x10 thin outline (matches Java space bar); gaps 3->2. Settings MOD PREVIEW demo uses the same classes -> automatically matches.

## 2026-10-09 session (round 4) — W2 web-everywhere HUD: the web widgets ARE the game HUD (WEB_VERSION 27 -> 28)

### Decision (user rejected round 3)

Round 3 (Java draws the widgets under an outline-only web ghost) was tried and the user rejected it: *"still fully glitched... can't we use web widgets instead of java?"*. **W2 architecture chosen: Java stops drawing widget pixels ENTIRELY. The exact same DOM widget elements the HUD editor shows are what renders in-game.** The round-3 ghost CSS block (dashed outline + `visibility:hidden` content) was DELETED and replaced.

Why it works: `InGameHud` (and therefore `HudRenderCallback`) renders UNDER Screens and fires every frame while a world is loaded. So `HudRenderer` can (a) push a data feed into the always-loaded overlay page, and (b) when NO screen is up, pump + draw the overlay page's TEXTURE under the game. The page is already DPR=1 with % positions => editor pixels == game pixels trivially.

### Feed (the single source of truth for body classes)

`HudRenderer.render(ctx)`:
1. Gates: `player/world != null`, `!hudHidden`; `overlayWindow()` must exist.
2. **`pushFeed(overlay, client)` runs ALWAYS** (even while chat/F3/menu screens are up) via `view.evaluateScript(...)` on the render thread:
   - First, standalone body-class toggles (correct BEFORE app.js even loads):
     `document.body.classList.toggle('hud-edit',` + (overlayOpen && isHudEditorActive()) + `);`
     `document.body.classList.toggle('hud-idle',` + (!overlayOpen) + `);`
     where `overlayOpen = (currentScreen instanceof WebScreen ws) && ws.mode() == OVERLAY`.
   - Then `window.__batHud && window.__batHud(` + GSON JSON + `);`
3. **Pump + draw only when** `client.getOverlay() == null && !(currentScreen instanceof WebScreen)`: `WebController.INSTANCE.adoptWindow(overlay)` (whoever pumps owns the adopt — fixes the stale-pause-window bug), `WebController.updateAndRender()`, `overlay.draw(ctx)` guarded by `hasPainted()`. Logs `[hud] overlay HUD first frame drawn` once, `[hud] feed failed` once.
4. ALL GL/widget drawing code deleted (`fillRounded`, `pressFlash`, `drawFps`, plates, keys...). Class markers verified: `pushFeed` present, `fillRounded`/`pressFlash` ABSENT from `HudRenderer.class`.

Feed JSON (only enabled modules; formatting split Java/JS):
- Java-side string formatting (settings `labels`, potion `format` Shorthand/Full + roman numerals): `fps` ("144 FPS"), `coords` (with labels), `ping` (omitted if null), `cps` ("LMB n CPS   RMB n CPS"), `sprint` bool, `potions` [{n,t}], `armor` ["363",...] ("" if not damageable), `dur` ["98%",...] ("" if not damageable), `target` {name, hp "%.1f / %.1f"} (omitted if no target), `keys` {w,a,s,d,lmb,rmb,space,lc,rc}.
- **Three body modes**: `hud-idle` (overlay screen closed = live game: only widgets — transparent dim, pause-rail/overlay-center/mm-layer hidden, gear/hide/label/rz chrome hidden, widgets `pointer-events:none`, `data-live="0"` widgets hidden); `hud-edit` (overlay open + editor flag: widgets + chrome + dim 0.12); neither = menu (overlay open, editor off: chrome-less widgets over the normal 0.55 dim).

### Settings split (must stay this way)

- **JS-side styling** at widget build (`renderHudWidgets`): `background:false`->`no-bg`, `shadow:false`->`no-shadow`, `numbers:false`->`no-num`, `showCps:false`->`nocps`, `border:false`->`noborder`, `color`->CSS var `--hpc`, `pressedColor`->`--kflash` (rgba .33 = old Java 0x55 alpha), crosshair `style`->glyph (Dot=`&#9679;`, else `&#10010;`) via `widgetPreview(m,s)`.
- **Java-side** only text formatting (labels, potion format, roman).
- Helpers added in app.js: `hpColor(n)` (White/Gray/Green/Yellow/Aqua/Black hex table), `kflashColor(n)` (hex->rgba .33), `widgetPreview(m,s)`.

### Per-file changes

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `HudRenderer.java` | REWRITTEN: feed + pump/adopt + draw-texture; zero widget GL. Helpers kept: `resolve`, `coords`, `ping`, `potions`, `armorNumbers`, `durabilityPcts`, `target`, `keys`, `roman`, `armorStacks`. |
| `BatClientMod.java` | `WebWindow` import; `cachedOverlayScreen` + `setCachedOverlay/cachedOverlay/overlayWindow/isCachedWindow`; `hudEditorActive` comment updated; tick = **proactive** `createScreen(OVERLAY)` on world join (`world!=null && currentScreen==null && cache empty && isReady()`, log `[hud] overlay HUD window ready`) + world-null destroys BOTH caches (skip if currentScreen) via new `WebScreen.destroyWindow()`; `toggle()` reuses cached overlay in-world (adopt before setScreen; title path unchanged). |

| `app.js` | `renderHudWidgets` ALWAYS renders (old `hud-edit` early-return removed) + `data-id`/`data-live`/config classes/`--hpc`/`--kflash`/`widgetPreview`; new `window.__batHud(o)` + `hudEl/hudText/hudRows/hudEsc` (textContent only-when-changed, rows rebuilt on `dataset.sig`, target innerHTML esc+`<br>`, keystroke `.on` toggles + lc/rc counts, sets `data-live`); keystrokes PREVIEW gained `data-k` attrs; `renderMsPreview` parity (`--hpc` + crosshair glyph); `toggleHudEditor` comment rewritten (feed echoes flag back as body classes); init now `renderHudWidgets(); toggleHudEditor(true);`. |

### Sky glitch (reported alongside) — cause = FULLBRIGHT, fix DEFERRED

- Screenshots showed a soft square halo around the moon / wrong-looking night sky while fullbright was on.
- Cause (user-confirmed, verbatim: *"Full bright was causing it"*): `ClientMods` fullbright applies a fake `NIGHT_VISION` status effect (400 ticks, re-added when absent, ClientMods.java:116-118) — that is what artifacts the sky (the "Night Vision 0:00" row in the widget is OUR effect, not a potion).
- **Fix SKIPPED per user instruction ("first off all skip it").** -> **OPEN ITEM: rework fullbright to gamma/brightness-based (no fake status effect) to remove the sky halo.** Diagnostic breadcrumbs kept: no glow texture in vanilla `textures/environment/` set; only inactive resource packs (SodiumTranslations active only); `WebWindow.draw` restores `RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc()` (blend left enabled as before — not the cause).

| `--hbg` var | renderHudWidgets + renderMsPreview set `--hbg` from `s.bgColor`; appended CSS `.hud-wbox { background: var(--hbg, rgba(8,8,11,.78)) }` (+ hover keeps custom color, border still blue). |

Layout: title (uppercase), SV square 230px (hue-base + white/black gradients, circular cursor), vertical hue strip, vertical alpha strip (checker + `--cp-rgb` gradient), right column = Reset color / Selected color checker box / hex input (6 or 8 chars) / HEXA caption, under: Favorite Colors (10 fixed Bat swatches incl. plate #08080BC7) + Recent Colors (localStorage `bat.colorrecents`, max 10, pushed on close). Value format `#RRGGBBAA`; helpers `hexToHsva`/`hsvaToHex`. `mountModControl` gained `type:"color"` case: swatch button (checker + backgroundColor so alpha reads) -> picker; `onChange` live-updates `st.set`, swatch, renderMsPreview, renderHudWidgets (NO save per frame); `onCommit` (popup close) does saveModState. ESC ladder: `closeColorPicker()` is FIRST (returns true when open). Veil mousedown outside = close. Default value bug fixed: color rows fell into `row.options[0]` crash path -> default from `msMod.defaults[key]`. |
| WebKit trap avoided | SV/strips use `pointerdown`+`setPointerCapture` NOT `mousedown` — MouseEvent has no pointerId in WebKit (Ultralight); would throw. |

## 2026-10-10 session (round 6) - Native Java HUD + windowed mod menu + FULL layout engine (WEB_VERSION 29 -> 30)

### A. Native HUD renderer (Pipeline A) - replaced W2 web-everywhere

User rejected W2 (web texture HUD). Restored native Java drawing (Feather/Lunar style):

- **`HudRenderer.java` REWRITTEN**: no WebWindow/WebController/pump/`__batHud` feed. Draws whenever `player/world != null && !hudHidden` (even under Screens so the editor shows real pixels). Gates: togglesprint (only when sprinting), ping (network entry), target (LivingEntity), potions/armor/durability (non-empty).
- **Matrix isolation per module**: push -> `translate(absX,absY)` -> `scale(s,s,1)` -> draw local 0,0 -> pop.
- **Plate geometry**: padH=6, padV=4, radius=4, default plate `0xC708080B`, bg from `set.bgColor` (`#RRGGBBAA`), text from `set.color`, shadow from `set.shadow`. `fillRounded` = center strips + per-row sqrt corners.
- **Keystrokes**: content 74x62, plate 86x70; background on = ONE plate + `pressFlash` (r=3, alpha forced 0x55); space = thin `0x44FFFFFF` outline; background off = classic per-key boxes. LMB/RMB 36x14 with optional CPS counters.
- **Crosshair**: 22x22 plate hides vanilla cross; Cross / Cross+Dot / Dot from `set.style`.
- **parseColor**: `#RRGGBBAA` / `#RRGGBB` / legacy names (White/Gray/Green/Yellow/Aqua/Black/Red/Blue).

### B. Windowed Feather mod menu

- `overlay.html` + `pause.html`: wrapped `.mm-head + .mm-tabs + .mm-body` in new **`.mm-win`** card.
- `style.css` round-6 block (file end): `.mm-layer` = translucent dim + blur + padding (world visible); `.mm-win` = centered card max-width 1280, radius 18, `#0d0d11`, shadow; `.mm-body/.mm-panel` fill the card. Editor ghost: `body.hud-edit .hud-wbox` transparent + dashed blue border + padding 4/6; `.hp/.keys` `visibility:hidden` (layout kept for drag sizing); default `.hud-widget { display:none }` + `body.hud-edit .hud-widget { display:flex }` (Java is the in-game HUD).

### C. Full HUD layout engine (user spec: dual pipeline + anchors + snapping)

New package **`com.batclient.hud`**:

| Class | Role |
|---|---|
| `layout/AnchorPoint` | 9-point enum (TOP_LEFT..BOTTOM_RIGHT) with `anchorX/Y(W,H)` |
| `layout/HudComponentState` | Mutable schema: id/enabled/scale/anchor/offset_x/offset_y + cached base size |
| `layout/HudLayoutConfig` | Root JSON: canvas (base 1920x1080, global_gui_scale, corner_radius) + modules[]; Gson round-trip + JsonObject bridge |
| `layout/AbsoluteBounds` | Float AABB (minX/Y, maxX/Y, contains/overlaps/area/center) |
| `layout/HudCoordinateResolver` | Pure math: `computeAbsoluteBounds(state, contentW/H, screenW/H)`, `setAbsoluteTopLeft`, `reanchorPreservingPosition`, `clampTopLeftToScreen` |
| `snap/SnapResult` | finalAbsX/Y + center/edge guide flags |
| `snap/HudSnappingEngine` | SNAP_THRESHOLD=5px; screen-center axes then inter-module edges (smallest \|delta\| wins); `applyToState` re-derives offsets = finalAbs - Ax |
| `editor/HudDragSession` | grabOffset = mouse - offset (no origin snap); `rawAbsX/Y(mouse)` |
| `editor/HudEditorController` | Pipeline B input: pick (smallest area, then highest index), drag, snap, commit callback; FrameState DEFAULT/HOVERED/DRAGGED |
| `editor/HudEditorScreen` | `Screen` subclass: R-Shift toggle, mouse intercept, dotted guides, scroll = scale 0.5-3.0, Esc/close persists |
| `HudLayoutEngine` | Bridge HudConfig <-> typed states; **legacy percent x/y -> TOP_LEFT pixels** migration on load; persist writes anchor schema + legacy x/y percent mirror |

**Pipeline A** (`HudRenderer`): loads states via `HudLayoutEngine.loadStates(sw,sh)`, measures content, resolves AABB, matrix-isolated draw. Skips when `HudEditorScreen` is open (Pipeline B owns pixels).

**Pipeline B** (`HudEditorScreen`): Right-Shift (`key.batclient.hudeditor`, lang added). Drag uses `HudEditorController` + `HudSnappingEngine`. Gray/blue/green frames; center + edge dotted guides.

**Smoke test** `scripts/HudLayoutSmoke.java`: anchors, bounds, snap center/edge/guide, JSON round-trip, drag session — **ALL_SMOKE_OK**.

### Round 6c (same session) — editor = web widgets (Feather), lag fixed, WEB_VERSION 32

User: "the outline and widget doesn't have the same shape and it is laggy the hud editor and use webgui for widget background like feather"

**Root causes**
1. Ghost outline (dashed transparent border + hidden content) never matched the Java plate underneath — different DOM box vs Java fillRounded geometry.
2. Lag: Java HudRenderer still ran Pipeline A every frame while the overlay was up, AND (from earlier W2) would have been evaluateScript every frame — double GPU + JS churn.

**Fix (v32)**
- `HudRenderer.render()`: when `BatClientMod.isHudEditorActive()` → **return immediately after pushing a throttled live feed** (no Java plate, no matrix draw). Web widgets ARE the HUD in the editor.
- New `pushFeedThrottled(overlay, client)`: >=100ms between evaluates (~10Hz), builds the same `__batHud` payload as the old every-frame feed (`fps/coords/ping/cps/sprint/potions[]/armor[]/dur[]/target/keys`). Helpers: `potionsFeed`, `armorFeed`, `durabilityFeed`, `keysFeed`. Errors logged once.
- style.css: `body.hud-edit .hud-wbox` is no longer a ghost — real dark plate (`var(--hbg, rgba(8,8,11,.78))`), blue border 0.35 → full #4da3ff on hover/drag, content `visibility:visible`, subtle drop shadow. Outline and plate are the **same element** — shape can never disagree.
- `WEB_VERSION` 31 → **32**. compile EXITCODE=0. Boot: `version.txt=32`, `[bat] init ok`, 0 ERROR / 0 Exception. Client left running.

**Behavior after**
- Open overlay / HUD editor → full Feather-style widgets with live values, drag/resize, hover chrome. No double-draw, no ghost mismatch.
- Close editor → Java Pipeline A draws the same config for gameplay.

## 2026-10-11 — Web menu render performance (assets v54)

- User reported lag in Right Shift and Bat mod menus. Recorded a 20-second JFR capture at build/menu-before.jfr; Java execution samples included world-tick activity, so this is not an isolated before/after menu FPS benchmark.
- Source audit found native editor still sending obsolete live HUD values and reapplying DOM geometry at 20Hz; native settings previews ran an idle requestAnimationFrame loop; web compositor ran at unrestricted Minecraft FPS; full-width surface uploads copied row by row.
- HudRenderer feed now sends only config/mode/native measurements, and identical payloads skip the JavaScript bridge. Repeated identical dimensions cause no widget style changes. Live text/icons remain Java-rendered.
- Web compositor is paced at 60Hz independently of game FPS, retaining deadline phase so 90/144/240 FPS gameplay does not round UI down to half rate. Input events invalidate the deadline; loading, page activation, queued callbacks and resizing bypass the cap.
- View resize now precedes paint/upload. Full-width contiguous dirty regions use one staging-buffer copy rather than per-row calls. Existing padded staging, exact-size PBO allocation and driver pixel-store safety remain.
- Native preview layout callbacks stop when entrance motion ends; scrolling, resizing, picker and preview visibility changes schedule updates. Unchanged clocks do not rewrite text.
- Validation: offline Gradle build successful; overlay 289 checks, pause 25; UiFrameClockSmoke confirms target cadence at 30/60/90/144/240 FPS, immediate invalidation, loading bypass and no catch-up bursts. - **Cached 1.21.11 Merged JARs:**
  - Fabric: `D:/BatClientBuildCache/gradle-home/caches/fabric-loom/minecraftMaven/net/minecraft/minecraft-merged/1.21.11-net.fabricmc.yarn.1_21_11.1.21.11+build.6-v2/minecraft-merged-1.21.11-net.fabricmc.yarn.1_21_11.1.21.11+build.6-v2.jar`

- **World Nametags (`PlayerEntityRenderer` & `OrderedRenderCommandQueue`):**
  - `PlayerEntityRenderer.renderLabelIfPresent(PlayerEntityRenderState, MatrixStack, OrderedRenderCommandQueue, CameraRenderState)` does not call `super.renderLabelIfPresent`; it calls `OrderedRenderCommandQueue.submitLabel(MatrixStack, Vec3d, int, Text, boolean, int, double, CameraRenderState)` directly.
  - `LabelCommandRenderer.Commands.add` expands `submitLabel` into `matrices.translate(pos.x, pos.y + 0.5, pos.z)`, `matrices.multiply(camera.orientation)`, `matrices.scale(0.025F, -0.025F, 0.025F)`, and `submitText(...)` calls for `SEE_THROUGH` + `NORMAL` (with `LightmapTextureManager.applyEmission(light, 2)` when `notSneaking`).
  - `NativeNametagStyleMixin` in `scripts/adapter_12111.py` now targets `@Mixin(PlayerEntityRenderer.class)` and `@Redirect`s `OrderedRenderCommandQueue.submitLabel` inside `renderLabelIfPresent` to `NativeNametags.submitLabel(...)`.
  - `NativeNametags.submitLabel` and `NativeNametags.drawLogo` in `scripts/adapter_12111.py` use the canonical `src/main/java/com/batclient/NativeNametags.java` helpers (`enabled()`, `settings()`, `hasLogo(text)`, `HudConfig.setNumber(sMap, "scale", 1)`, `HudConfig.bool(sMap, "background", true)`, `HudConfig.bool(sMap, "shadow", true)`, `LOGO` with UV `(0.10f, 0.31f)` to `(0.90f, 0.69f)` at `w=16f, h=8f, z=0.05f` submitted via `queue.submitCustom(matrices, renderLayer, ...)`).
- **Chat HUD (`ChatHud` & `ChatHud.Backend`):**
  - In 1.21.11, `ChatHud.render` has signature `(DrawContext, TextRenderer, int ticks, int mouseX, int mouseY, boolean focused, boolean insert)V` and delegates to `private void render(ChatHud.Backend, int scaledWindowHeight, int ticks, boolean focused)`.
  - `toChatLineX`, `toChatLineY`, and `getMessageLineIndex` were removed from `ChatHud` in 1.21.11 (replaced by `ChatHud.Interactable`), while `getChatScale()`, `getWidth()`, `getLineHeight()`, `getVisibleLineCount()`, `scrolledLines`, and `visibleMessages` remain on `ChatHud`.
  - `ChatHudMixin` and `NativeChat` in `scripts/adapter_12111.py` now match the canonical `src/main/java` instance API: `ChatCopyAccess.bat$copyAt(double, double)` returns `String` via `bat$state().copyText(visibleMessages.get(index).content())`; `bat$visible`, `bat$refreshBegin`, `bat$refreshEnd`, `bat$clear`, `bat$configuration`, and `bat$endOpening` are preserved; and `@ModifyVariable(method = "render(Lnet/minecraft/client/gui/hud/ChatHud$Backend;IIZ)V", at = @At("HEAD"), argsOnly = true)` wraps `ChatHud.Backend` via `bat$state().wrapBackend(delegate, bat$context, client.textRenderer, isChatFocused(), scrolledLines > 0)`, calling `NativeChat.draw(ctx, font, text, 0, y, color, focused, scrolled)`.
- **Forge 1.21.11 (`61.2.4`) World & Client Hooks:**
  - `LevelStageEvent` is removed in Forge 61.2.4; `WorldRenderer.drawBlockOutline(MatrixStack, VertexConsumer, double, double, double, WorldRenderState, int, float)` is `private` and fires `ForgeHooksClient.onDrawHighlight(WorldRenderer, Camera, WorldRenderState, MatrixStack, VertexConsumerProvider$Immediate)`.
  - `scripts/adapter_12111.py` now adapts `ForgeFullSmoke.java`, `ForgeWorldStageMixin.java`, `ForgeWorldFrame.java`, `ForgeClientHooks.java`, and `ForgeClientResources.java` for 1.21.11:
    - `ForgeWorldStageMixin` injects at `@At("RETURN")` on `WorldRenderer.render` (`(Lnet/minecraft/client/util/ObjectAllocator;Lnet/minecraft/client/render/RenderTickCounter;ZLnet/minecraft/client/render/Camera;Lorg/joml/Matrix4f;Lorg/joml/Matrix4f;Lorg/joml/Matrix4f;Lcom/mojang/blaze3d/buffers/GpuBufferSlice;Lorg/joml/Vector4f;Z)V`) to run `WorldVisualModules.onWorldLast(ForgeWorldFrame.context(camera, positionMatrix))` and `@At("HEAD")` on `WorldRenderer.drawBlockOutline` (`cancellable = true`) to run `WorldVisualModules.onBlockOutline(ForgeWorldFrame.context(client.gameRenderer.getCamera(), matrices), ForgeWorldFrame.hitResult())`.
    - Note on Forge 61.2.4 Loom remap (`ports/forge-1.21.11/PORT-STATUS.md`): Loom remap previously hit a duplicate named `BucketItem.getFluid` signature (`class_1755.method_74140` vs Forge-added `getFluid()Lnet/minecraft/class_3611;`); check/apply a Tiny mapping override in `ports/forge-1.21.11/build.gradle` if `:remapJar` or Loom configuration hits that duplicate name.

## 2026-11-14 — 1.8.x & nofapi Fabric fixes + port audit kickoff

- **nofapi Fabric resource loading (pink/black crosshair & missing `batclient:*` textures):**
  - Added `BatClasspathResourcePack.java`, `NofapiBatResourcePackMixin.java`, and `batclient.nofapi.mixins.json` to `ports/fabric-1.8.9`, `ports/fabric-1.8`, `ports/fabric-1.16.5`, and `ports/fabric-1.17.1` to expose `/assets/batclient/...` via `ReloadableResourceManagerImpl.reload`.
  - Excluded `NofapiBatResourcePackMixin.java` from `ports/forge-1.8.9/build.gradle` and `ports/forge-1.8/build.gradle` (Forge loads `@Mod` resource packs via FML).
- **Pre-1.9 fake HUD modules & singleplayer ping (`HudRenderer.java` + `ports/mc1.8.9/src/com/batclient/HudRenderer.java`):**
  - Skipped `totems`, `elytrahud`, and `shieldindicator` on pre-1.9 clients (`SharedConstants` / `1.8.x` override) so `totems` does not render a golden apple fallback.
  - Returned `"0 ms"` in singleplayer (`c.isInSingleplayer()`) and clamped invalid latency (`< 0 || > 30000`) to avoid `1532139 ms`.
  - Enabled `GlStateManager.enableBlend()` / `blendFunc(770, 771)` in `mc1.8.9/HudRenderer.java::drawCrosshair`.
- **1.8.x Module Preview scissor fix (`ports/mc1.8.9/src/com/batclient/platform/mc1194/DrawContext.java`):**
  - Fixed `enableScissor` using scaled `win.getHeight()` instead of framebuffer `client.height` in `GL11.glScissor`, which previously clipped out the entire Module Preview box.
- **1.8.x `IllegalStateException: Already building!` crash (`ports/mc1.8.9/src/com/batclient/VisualLayers.java` & `WorldVisualModules.java`):**
  - `beginPlate`/`endPlate` and `beginLines`/`endLines` called `buf.begin`/`tess.draw()` while `WaypointRenderer`, `LootBeams`, and `WorldVisualModules` also started/ended tessellation or called `WorldRenderer.drawBox`.
  - Changed `beginPlate`/`endPlate` to GL-state-only and added GL-state-only `beginLineState`/`endLineState` (keeping `beginLines`/`endLines` for `ReturnTrail`), updating `WorldVisualModules.java` accordingly.
